# v0.22.0 — READ RECEIPTS, a dating simulation on a phone of apps — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** A seventh story, `read-receipts`, played entirely on a phone: a player profile, two dating apps with different cultures, Texts and Natter, voice notes, selfies and video messages, two two-player games, meet-ups, and a generated cast the engine runs from the seed while the model only gives them their words -- with no ending.

**Architecture:**
- One manifest key, `paths.social`, switches on a generic engine package, `engine/social/`. A story that does not declare it pays nothing: no phone route, no `phone_action` event, no `phone` payload key, its turns byte-identical.
- The phone turn is TWO things that never hold the same lock at once: a fast **mechanics action** (`turn.run_mechanics` under `run_guarded(..., runner=, admit=False, metric="phone")`, no model, no admission) and a background **voicing pass** (`voicer.VoicingScheduler`) that snapshots under the turn lock, calls the model holding no lock, and commits each reply under the lock again with re-validation.
- Every mechanic is an engine decision from the seed and the player's structured `phone_action` (rule 1): events on an exact-time queue walked inside `clock.advance_time` (rule 2), all writes through `effects.apply_effect` with engine-only `social_*` kinds (rule 3), all draws through named streams (rule 4). Media go through a new ComfyUI package (`engine/media/comfy/`) on a priority worker; TTS through the existing Voxtral route with its cache key fixed.
- The client gains two plugin slots (`PlayScreen`, `NewRun`), one outbound event (`phone_action`) and two inbound (`phone_message`, `media_ready`); the phone itself is the plugin `ui/src/stories/read-receipts/`.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.11, Flask 3.1 + Flask-SocketIO 5.6, httpx, PyYAML (libyaml), pytest + pytest-xdist + pytest-timeout; React 18, Vite 5, vitest 2 + jsdom, socket.io-client 4.8.3, playwright-core (layout check, already a devDependency). Client tests follow the repo's convention (`ui/tests/job-panel.test.jsx`): `// @vitest-environment jsdom` on the first line of each file, `createRoot` plus `React.act`, and the shared helpers in `ui/tests/helpers/dom.js` (T25). **No `@testing-library/react`**: it is not a dependency and none is added (pre-flight F4).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-08-v0.22.0-dating-sim-design.md` (Revision 2, committed in cb6a5e2). Section numbers (§N), finding numbers (F1-F16) and task numbers (T1-T32) below are the spec's. The owner's brief, the authority for WHAT is built: `docs/superpowers/briefs/2026-09-30-dating-sim-brief.md`. Executors read the spec and this plan; where they disagree, the spec wins unless a **Plan decision** below says why not.

## Global Constraints

The spec has no section titled "Global Constraints"; these are its project-wide requirements, quoted **verbatim** (marked "spec") or taken from the controller's standing instructions and AGENTS.md (marked "standing"). Every task's requirements implicitly include this whole section.

- (spec, Non-goals) "**Any content-rating, intensity, boundary or "safety" layer, in any form** (AGENTS.md rule 12). Not in the texter prompt, the summary prompt or the date recap, not in the ComfyUI request builder, not in any drafting prompt (`scripts/author.py` gets no new prompt for this story), not as a "declines explicit requests" behaviour. A person declines a request because of WHERE THE RELATIONSHIP IS (stage, trust, mood, budget, how often they have been asked, their personality) -- never because of what was asked for. The register belongs to `games/read-receipts/prompts/texter.md` and the owner's model. §11.6 pins every composed prompt's blocks by test."
- (spec §13.3) "**No negative prompt** (v0.15.1), **no sanitiser, no classifier** (rule 12): the only processing is the length cap."
- (spec, Goal) "**One release.** Everything above ships in v0.22.0 (§24). There is no v0.22.1."
- (spec, Goal) "Every one of those is an ENGINE mechanic resolved from the seed and the player's structured actions (rule 1). The model writes what people SAY, and proposes -- inside engine bounds -- how each exchange moved them."
- (spec §2.1) "A story that declares no `paths.social` pays nothing: `engine/social` is never imported on its turns, its app registers no phone route or event, its payload carries no `phone` key, and its turns stay byte-identical, asserted by test for every shipped story."
- (spec §2.1) "Every content fault is a `ValueError` naming file and key, raised at load (the `agendas.py` rule)".
- (spec §20) "Rule 10 covers every number in `behaviours.yaml`, `apps.yaml` (its `matching:` block included), `relationships.yaml` (`regard:`, `interest:`, `mood:`, `asks:`, `meets:`), `media.yaml` and `games.yaml`. Engine bounds (caps, windows, budgets of work) are config under `social.*`, documented in `config/default.yaml`."
- (spec §5.1) "Data headers read **"unmeasured until T26"** until the full simulator pass writes the measured tables."
- (spec §19) "No live model: the voicing call is stubbed (`tests/social_stub.py`, a scripted `llm_fn` returning schema-valid replies by policy, optionally blocked on an event), `_no_live_model_calls` stays default-deny, ComfyUI is `StubComfy`."
- (spec §24) "Each task lands green on its own (`run_tests.py files` for what it touches, `npm test` when `ui/src` changes, `dist` rebuilt and committed with it), with its docs, reviewed before the next starts, subagent-driven. ... **No task needs a later one**: each test lands in the task that builds what it tests, and a shared function (`run_mechanics`, `test_social_intents.py`, the agreement checks) gains its steps, rows or checks in the task that builds each part."
- (spec §19.6) "Any suite-wide "every story ..." test the new story fails is fixed by the story or carries a declared, tested reason -- never a skip."
- (spec, Acceptance) "The six other stories' payloads and turns byte-identical; the local golden green with exactly the one sanctioned `games.json` row; `app_shape.json` and the LM Studio golden untouched."
- (standing) **AGENTS.md's twelve rules bind every task.** Rule 1 (intents; a refusal reaches the player, never a silent no-op), rule 2 (`clock.advance_time` only), rule 3 (`effects.apply_effect` only), rule 4 (`world_rng`/`stable_rng`, never bare `random`), rule 5 (nothing hardcoded: `get_config()`), rule 6 (never gate rest), rule 9 (NOT WIRED rows with their files), rule 10 (`scripts/simulate_social.py` before a balance constant changes), rule 11 (platform-neutral: no path separator, drive letter, `.exe`; `pathlib` only), rule 12 (above).
- (standing) **Tests run per the repo's methodology.** A task runs only the files it touches: `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\run_tests.py files <paths>` (PowerShell; Git Bash: `.venv/Scripts/python.exe scripts/run_tests.py files <paths>`). Never the full suite inside a task. Never a hand-built long `pytest` line. T32 alone runs `scripts\run_tests.py full`, once. `npm test --prefix ui` when `ui/src` or `ui/tests` changes.
- (standing) **Never touch the owner's files or services.** Never read or write `config/local.yaml` or the real `data/` (tests use `tmp_path`, `CLOCKWORK_DATA_DIR`, `tests/hosted_app.py`, and a patched `engine.config._CONFIG_DIR`); never touch `C:\Users\Knack\.cache\lm-studio\mcp.json`; never call a live model in a test (stub `llm_fn`; `_no_live_model_calls` is default-deny); never load or unload a model; never signal a process by bare pid (tests use `tests/process_identity.py`; a background server is started with the Bash tool's `run_in_background` or `--exit-with-parent` and stopped with `TaskStop`).
- (standing) **Downloads.** The owner's standing approval covers downloads (memory, 2026-10-06). The one download in this release is the ENABLE word list (T22), recorded with its source URL, size and licence.
- (standing) **Module state is classified.** Every new module-level global, class-level container, `lru_cache`, lock, thread, timer or pool site under `engine/` gets its row in `tests/fixtures/module_state.yaml` in the same task; every new lock joins `engine/locks.py` (ordered list or leaf list) and `tests/lock_order.py` and is registered with `renew_after_fork`; every cache in `NULLED_ATTRIBUTES` is read once into a local.
- (standing) **The client build is committed.** Any `ui/src` change runs `npm run build --prefix ui` and commits `content/scenes/clockwork/static/dist` IN THE SAME COMMIT.
- (standing) **Docs move with the change** (AGENTS.md): in the same commit, every doc the change makes stale -- `games/read-receipts/CHANGELOG.md` `## [Unreleased]` and its README, the root `CHANGELOG.md` `## [Unreleased]` and `README.md` for engine changes, `docs/AUTHORING.md`, `docs/GOVERNANCE.md` (NOT WIRED rows), `docs/DESIGN.md`, `docs/HOSTING.md`, `docs/MEDIA_SERVERS.md` (new, T19), `CLAUDE.md` when status, roadmap or the deferred list moves. "Updated" means stale sentences removed, not appended to.
- (standing) **Git.** Branch `release/v0.22.0` (checked out). Commit messages inside the branch are free-form; write each to `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-08-v0.22.0-read-receipts/commit-t<N>-msg.txt` (gitignored) with the editor tool and commit with `git commit -F <file>` (heredocs and `python -c` lose backticks on this machine). Every message ends with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`. Describe engine SCOPE, never a content-policy decision. **Nothing is pushed.** The final squash to `main` uses the bare subject `v0.22.0` (T32).
- (standing) **Tests.** A fix ships with a test that failed before it; a new guard is canary-checked by reintroducing what it guards (and a canary that removes a guard runs only after the home directory, `_CONFIG_DIR`, the storage root and the model endpoints are redirected into the test's temp directory). A test that starts an interpreter is marked `process`; one that binds a loopback server is marked `loopback`; a balance measurement is `slow`.

## Plan decisions (where the spec is silent, or the code disagrees with it)

Each is repeated, marked **Plan decision N**, at the step it governs.

1. **No "Global Constraints" section in the spec.** The section above quotes the spec's project-wide lines verbatim and adds the standing instructions, each labelled.
2. **"`engine/social` is never imported" means its SUBMODULES.** `clock.advance_time`, `GameState.to_client_dict` and `default_scene.register` must ask whether the story declares `paths.social`; the asking lives in `engine/social/__init__.py`, which imports nothing but `engine.config` (the `law.declared()` pattern, `engine/world/law.py:114`). The seam test in T1 asserts that a full turn of each of the six shipped stories ADDS no `engine.social.<anything>` name to `sys.modules` (a set snapshot taken before the turn, compared after -- never a `sys.modules.pop`, which builds second copies of every registry and singleton and pollutes the xdist worker, pre-flight F8), and one `process` test proves "never imported" honestly, in a fresh interpreter. `engine.skills.builtin.social` imports nothing of `engine.social` at module top (its skills import lazily).
3. **The `games.json` sanction lives in `tests/test_local_mode_golden.py`'s `SANCTIONED` list** (spec §19.6 says `tests/local_golden.py`; that file is the recorder, the sanctions are in the test module, `:470`). One `json_sanction` that inserts the READ RECEIPTS row where the registry's catalogue order puts it and asserts nothing else moved.
4. **Phone skills are system-only.** Each `social_*` skill is registered `pack="social"`, `category="PHONE"`, `trigger=TRIGGER_SYSTEM`, `agents=[AGENT_SYSTEM]`, so the storyteller's tool list, the MCP skills server's per-agent list and the LM Studio golden do not move. `tests/test_reachability.py::test_every_registered_skill_is_reachable_or_declared_unreachable` only judges storyteller GAME skills, so T6 adds `test_every_phone_skill_has_a_phone_verb` beside it: every registered skill with `pack == "social"` is a value of `engine.social.intents.PHONE_SKILL_FOR_ACTION`, and every value is registered. No allowlist row is added (spec §7.3).
5. **`_autosave` gains `record: bool = True` and `entry: Optional[dict] = None`.** A phone save made by the dirty timer, a flush or a pass passes `record=False` (no transcript line per save); the mechanics action passes `entry={"phone": action, "day", "hour"}`; the away advance passes `entry={"kind": "away", "hours", "real_seconds"}` (spec §15.2). Existing callers are byte-identical.
6. **The summarizer's "new `renderer` parameter" is a new lower function**, `engine/memory/summarizer.py::summarize_with(existing, items, *, renderer, fallback, system, new_label, llm_fn, max_words) -> tuple[str, bool]`; `summarize` is rewritten to call it with `_render_turns`, `_fallback_summary`, `SUMMARIZER_PROMPT` and `"NEW EVENTS"`, producing byte-identical messages (the LM Studio golden pins them). The thread summary passes its own renderer, its own deterministic fallback, `prompts/thread_summary.md` and `"NEW MESSAGES"`.
7. **`run_mechanics` takes `run_turn`'s calling shape**, `run_mechanics(session, action, intent=None, *, authored=None, emit_callback=None) -> dict`, so `run_guarded` calls whichever runner it was given identically. `action` is the phone action's player label ("Swipe right on Kai"); `intent` is the normalised `phone_action` body.
8. **The phone metric's hook mirrors `turn_metric`.** `engine/scenes/default_scene.py` gains `phone_metric: Optional[Callable[[str, str, str, float], None]]` (`account, event, status, ms`); `engine/hosting/metrics_emit.py` gains `hook_phone` and `install_hooks` sets it. Local mode reports nothing, as for turns.
9. **The phone's rate bucket and caps need four hosting files the spec's T6 row omits:** `engine/hosting/config.py` (validator row and `HostingSettings.phone_actions_per_minute`), `engine/hosting/auth.py` (`HostingState.phone_actions`), `engine/hosting/gate.py` (`PHONE_RULES` for `POST /api/phone/action`) and `engine/hosting/__init__.py` (building the limiter). All in T6.
10. **No atomic bytes writer exists** (`engine/persistence/atomic.py` has `write_json_atomic`, `read_json`, `append_jsonl`). T18 adds `write_bytes_atomic(path, data)` beside them (temp file in the same directory, `os.replace`).
11. **The effect kinds are named once, in T1.** `engine/social/kinds.py::SOCIAL_EFFECT_KINDS` lists every engine-only kind the release uses, and `effects.ENGINE_ONLY_EFFECTS` includes them from T1. Each later task registers the handler for its own kinds in its own module (`@effects.effect_kind(...)`); T26 adds `test_every_social_kind_has_a_handler`. Until a kind's task lands nothing applies it, so no handler is a stub.
12. **The catalogue and the payload are registries.** `engine/social/intents.py::phone_verb(...)` registers a catalogue row and `engine/social/payload.py::section(name)` a payload section, each in the module that owns the mechanic; `engine/social/__init__.py::boot()` imports the submodules (each task appends its own import line). So T7-T24 add rows and sections without editing T6's or T10's functions, and no task leaves a stub (spec §16.1 "Each later task adds its step").
13. **T15's "first tables recorded in the plan's notes"** are appended to this file under `## Notes (written during execution)` at the end, in T15's commit; T26 writes the final tables into the data headers and the story CHANGELOG.
14. **Thread ids are `"<app>:<pid>"`** (`"natter:p0412"`), message ids `"m<n>"` from `state.social["counters"]["message"]`, request ids `"r<n>"`, media ids `"x<n>"`, game ids `"g<n>"`, meet ids `"t<n>"` -- all counters in state, so a replay names everything the same.
15. **The away advance runs inside the session's turn lock**, in `SessionStore.resume` after `_build` returns and before the resume frame is built, by `engine/social/away.py::apply_on_resume(session)`; it saves at once through `_autosave(..., entry=...)` (Plan decision 5).
16. **`Decision` lives in `engine/social/kinds.py`** (stdlib `dataclasses` only): T8 queues due messages carrying one before T13 writes `voicing.py`, which re-exports it. One class, two import paths.
17. **Stage writes before T9.** T8's number exchange writes the stage through `social_person`'s `set`; T9 introduces `social_stage`, moves every stage write to it and pins that with an AST scan (`test_every_stage_write_goes_through_social_stage`).
18. **The phone archive is written by the phone's one save path** (`saves.save_now`, under the turn lock, immediately before `_autosave` replaces the envelope), not inside `SaveStore.save` (spec §17.2): the spill must be an `apply_effect` (rule 3), which `SaveStore` -- a persistence layer that never writes state -- cannot make. The crash window and its dedupe are the spec's.
19. **Test helpers live in `tests/social_fixtures.py`**, created in T3 and growing task by task (T3 `receipts_active`/`receipts_state`; T6 `_engine`, `_act`, `hosted_receipts`; T7 `matched`, `_top`; T8 `matched_numbers`, `_set_clock`, `_force_stage`, `_set_person`; T9 `_force_archetype`, `_just_below`, `_at`, `_with`; T10 `_live_session`, `_phone`, `_matched_in`, `_wait_until`; T14 `_quiet_voicer`; T18 `media_root`, `_spec`, `_lines`; T24 `numbers_pid`), so a later task's test file imports what an earlier one wrote. ONE helper per job: the deck's top card is `_top(state, app)` everywhere (a session's is `_top(session.engine.state, app)`; there is no `_top_card`, pre-flight F21). The suite registers no `pytest_plugins`: each test module imports the fixtures it uses by name, and `_quiet_voicer` is applied through `receipts_active` and `hosted_receipts` (both request it), so no social test can miss it. Tests that import `scripts.*` put the repository root on `sys.path` first, as `tests/test_generate_art_cli.py:33` does.
20. **`create_app` returns `(scene, app)`** (`engine/scenes/default_scene.py:697`): every test here unpacks it.
21. **How tests are given.** Each task's critical tests are written out in full. Its further tests are listed as `def test_name(...) -> None: ...` lines followed by a paragraph stating each one's setup and assertion; the implementer writes those bodies from that paragraph, and the reviewer rejects a body that asserts less than the paragraph says. No test is left as `...` in the code.
22. **Block 1 of the voice prompt is `texter.md`'s persona section, verbatim** -- everything above its `## Instructions` heading. The spec says "`prompts/texter.md`, verbatim" and also keeps the instruction table and the fallbacks in that file (§11.3 item 6, §11.4); sending the whole file would put every situation line and every fallback into every call. `test_the_voice_prompt_is_exactly_its_blocks` pins the persona section byte for byte.

Decisions 23-32 were added by the pre-flight scan (`.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-08-v0.22.0-read-receipts/preflight.md`, rulings F1-F26, all accepted), before any task ran.

23. **`engine_requires: ">=0.2.0"`, not the spec's `">=0.22.0"`** (F1; a spec erratum in §2.3). `engine/games/__init__.py:32` is `ENGINE_VERSION = "0.2.0"`: the MANIFEST-CONTRACT version, not `pyproject.toml`'s, and every shipped manifest says `">=0.2.0"`. `registry.validate` (`registry.py:190`) refuses `">=0.22.0"`, so `registry.activate` would raise `ActivationError` and nothing from T2 on could go green. The spec confused the release number with the contract version. T1's probe manifest, T2's `game.yaml` and T32 all write `">=0.2.0"`.
24. **`agreement.check(reply, decision, view)` is pure** (F11; spec §11.5 writes `check(reply, decision, state, pid)`). The voicing call runs its checks on a snapshot holding no lock (T13, T14), so a check may not read `state`; `AgreementView` carries what the checks need. The spec's form is `check(reply, decision, view_from_state(state, pid))`, which is what T14's commit re-check calls. T11 builds `AgreementView`, `view_of`, `decision_of` AND `view_from_state` (all their state exists by T11); T13 adds only `view_from_snapshot`.
25. **T15 edits AGENTS.md** (F24; spec §25 says "AGENTS.md unchanged (no rule moved)"). The controller's ruling: `scripts/simulate_social.py` joins AGENTS.md's "Verify a checkout" block (both the PowerShell and the POSIX list), because that block is the convention's list of what a checkout runs. No rule moves; T32 checks the line instead of adding it.
26. **The phone's answer to `player_choice` is built from the active manifest's title** (F24; spec §7.2 quotes "Read Receipts is played on the phone"). `engine/social/turn.py` is story-agnostic engine code, so it holds no story's name: `played_on_the_phone() -> str` returns `f"{title} is played on the phone."` from `registry.peek().title`. For READ RECEIPTS (`title: "Read Receipts"`) it reads exactly as the spec quotes. There is no `PLAYED_ON_THE_PHONE` constant.
27. **The clock's import guard is narrower than spec §9.1's snippet** (F17). The spec wraps the whole call in `except ImportError: pass`, which would silently drop a declared story's whole event queue if a submodule failed to import: a phone that never replies, with no log. Only `from engine import social` (config-only) is guarded; once `social.declared()` is true, an `ImportError` from `boot()` or from importing `events` is logged at ERROR once per process (`engine.social.import_fault`, a `log_once` row) and that advance's social pass is skipped.
28. **Liveness asks the session's OWN store** (F3). The spec's generation check is `store.get(session.session_id, session.owner) is session` without saying which store. `default_scene.get_store()` is wrong for every store but the app's -- the simulator's, a test's hand-built one -- so every pass there read as cancelled and the simulator measured a model that never spoke. `GameSession` gains `store`, a back-reference set by `SessionStore._build`, and `saves.alive` uses it (an explicit `store=` still wins; a session built by hand with none falls back to the default store). A harness save store declaring `keeps_nothing = True` (T15's) makes `mark_dirty` schedule nothing and `save_now` write nothing, so a harness run starts no DirtySaver thread.
29. **T18 builds the video poster; T20 only wires video in** (F12). T18's `_run_one` already branches on a video output, so `MediaJobWorker._store_poster` and `tests/comfy_stub.py`'s `video` script land in T18 with unit tests on a hand-built video `Workflow`; T20 adds the `config/comfyui/video_wan.json` template, the `comfyui.workflows.video` config row and the social side. No task calls code a later task writes.
30. **A §20 row that cannot land is DEFERRED by data, never skipped and never widened** (F15; spec §19.6, Acceptance, AGENTS.md rule 8). T26 moves such a row from `BANDS` to `DEFERRED_ROWS` (row → the exact text of its CLAUDE.md deferred row, which T26 writes into CLAUDE.md in the same commit), and a test asserts that every `REPORT_ROWS` key is in exactly one of the two and that every deferred row's text is present in CLAUDE.md: a declared, tested reason, so the release stays fully green.
31. **One function releases the turn lock** (F21): `engine/session/store.py::release_turn(session)` (release, then the session's `end_after_turn`), re-exported as `engine.social.saves.release_turn`. `run_guarded`, `DirtySaver._run`, the voicer, `away.apply_on_resume` and the media hand-off all call it; T10 lifts `run_guarded`'s own `finally` into it, behaviour byte-identical. It lives in `store.py`, not under `engine/social/`, because `run_guarded` serves every story and must import no social submodule (spec §2.1).
32. **The phone block is built once per payload** (F21). `run_mechanics` builds `state.to_client_dict()` inside `payload.building(session, receipt)`, a context (a `ContextVar`) that `phone_block` reads when it is called with neither, so the block carries the session's typing marks and the receipt's refusal without being built twice.

## File Structure

### New engine package `engine/social/`

| File | Task | Responsibility |
|---|---|---|
| `engine/social/__init__.py` | T1 (+ one `boot()` import line per later task) | `declared()`, `social_dir()`, `data(name)` (cached YAML loader), `setting(key, default)`, `boot()`, `reset_cache()`, `import_fault(name, exc)` (T5, Plan decision 27) |
| `engine/social/kinds.py` | T1 (+ `Decision` in T8) | `SOCIAL_EFFECT_KINDS`, the state-slice skeleton `empty_slice()`, `Decision` |
| `engine/social/population.py` | T3 | `person(seed, index) -> Person`, `profile(state, pid)`, the per-seed LRU, the filter, pools |
| `engine/social/creation.py` | T4 | `resolved_form()`, `apply(state, payload)`, `CreationError` |
| `engine/social/behaviour.py` | T5 | `Archetype`, `archetype(name)`, `status_at(person, hour)`, `lognormal_minutes`, arcs |
| `engine/social/events.py` | T5 | `push(state, at, pid, kind, **data)`, `advance(state, from_hours, to_hours)`, `@handles(kind)` |
| `engine/social/intents.py` | T6 | `ArgSpec`, `phone_verb`, `legal_phone_intents`, `normalise_phone`, `phone_refusal`, `PHONE_SKILL_FOR_ACTION`, `PAYLOADS` |
| `engine/social/turn.py` | T6 (+T14 step 1, T19 step 3) | `run_mechanics`, `played_on_the_phone()` (Plan decision 26) |
| `engine/social/routes.py` | T4 (creation), T6 (action, registration), T10 (thread paging), T19 (media), T30 (the action log line) | `register(scene)`, the blueprint |
| `engine/social/payload.py` | T6 (core), T7-T29 add sections | `phone_block(state, session=None, *, receipt=None)`, `section(name, *, time_varying=False)`, `TIME_VARYING_SECTIONS`, `building(session, receipt)` |
| `engine/social/apps.py`, `matching.py` | T7 | apps table, decks, likes; compatibility, like-back, likes-you, expiry, unmatch/block |
| `engine/social/messaging.py` | T8 | threads, `send`, reads, replies, initiations, double texts, receipts, numbers |

| `engine/social/saves.py` | T10 | `alive`, `mark_dirty`, `keeps_nothing`, `DirtySaver`, `get_dirty_saver`, `reset_dirty_saver`, `save_now`, `flush_taken`, `flush_all`, `release_turn` (re-exported from `engine/session/store.py`), `IMMEDIATE` |

## Shared interfaces (every task uses these names exactly)

### The state slice: `GameState.social`

A plain `dict` (the `law` pattern, `engine/game/state.py:332`); absent loads as `{}`. `engine/social/kinds.py::empty_slice()` returns its skeleton, written by `social_profile` at creation:

```python
def empty_slice() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "player": {},            # creation.apply's profile (§3)
        "seq": 0,                # the queue's monotonic tie-break (§9.1)
        "queue": [],             # [{at, seq, pid, kind, ...}] kept sorted by (at, seq)
        "people": {},            # pid -> dynamic state only (§10.1)
        "threads": {},           # thread_id -> thread row (below)
        "notifications": [],     # newest last, at most social.notifications_max (30)
        "apps": {},              # app_id -> {likes_left, supers_left, day, deck: [pid...], deck_pos}
        "requests": {},          # request_id -> {id, pid, from, what, status, at, ...}
        "media": {},             # mid -> §13.2's job row
        "games": {},             # game_id -> game row
        "meets": {},             # meet_id -> meet row
        "settings": {"receipts": True, "auto_wait": False},
        "open_thread": "",       # the thread the player last opened
        "counters": {"message": 0, "request": 0, "media": 0, "game": 0, "meet": 0},
        "last_active_wall": 0.0, # wall seconds; stamped by every mechanics action and the away advance
        "dirty_reason": "",      # never read by a mechanic; the save trigger's last reason (debug)
    }
```

Thread row: `{"thread_id", "app", "pid", "messages": [msg...], "summary": "", "summary_through": "", "focus": 0, "unread": 0, "archived": 0, "opened_at": None}`.

Message row (`messaging.new_message`): `{"id", "thread_id", "from": "player" | pid, "kind": "text"|"voice"|"photo"|"video"|"system"|"request"|"recap", "at": float, "bubbles": [str...], "voiced": True | False | "fallback", "decision": {...} | None, "media_id": str | None, "read_at": float | None, "transcript": str | None}`. A player message is born `voiced: True`. A due message is born `voiced: False`, `bubbles: []`.

Decision (`engine/social/kinds.py::Decision`, re-exported as `voicing.Decision`; Plan decision 16; stored as its `asdict` in `message["decision"]`):

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Decision:
    situation: str                 # an id in texter.md's instruction table: opener, reply, double_text, ...
    kind: str = "text"             # text | voice | photo | video | decline | recap
    max_bubbles: int = 3
    media_kind: str = ""           # "", photo, voice, video -- set exactly when media is attached
    subject: str = ""              # a media.yaml subject id when media_kind is set
    reveal: str = ""               # an arc's canon row id (§5.3)
    request_id: str = ""           # the request this answers/declines
    game_id: str = ""              # game_banter
    meet_id: str = ""              # post_meet / recap
```

### Named streams (`engine/game/rng.py`, T1)

```python
SOCIAL_POPULATION = "social.population"   # stable, per person: stable_rng(seed, f"{SOCIAL_POPULATION}:{index}")
SOCIAL_MATCH = "social.match"             # once per pair: stable_rng(seed, f"{SOCIAL_MATCH}:{pid}")
SOCIAL_PACE = "social.pace"
SOCIAL_BEHAVIOUR = "social.behaviour"
SOCIAL_MEDIA = "social.media"
SOCIAL_MEET = "social.meet"
SOCIAL_GAME = "social.game"
```

Per-person draws (`engine/social/kinds.py` is data-only; the helper lives in `engine/social/behaviour.py`, T5):

```python
def person_rng(state: GameState, stream: str, pid: str) -> random.Random:
    """One draw on ``stream`` for ``pid``: its own counter in people[pid]["rng_n"][stream]."""
    person = state.social["people"].setdefault(pid, {})
    counters = person.setdefault("rng_n", {})
    n = int(counters.get(stream, 0)) + 1
    counters[stream] = n
    return stable_rng(state.rng_seed, f"{stream}:{pid}:{n}")
```

(Called only from inside a `social_*` effect handler, so the counter write is a state write under rule 3.)

### The phone intent (`engine/social/intents.py`, T6)

Wire shape of `phone_action` and `POST /api/phone/action`:

```json
{"session_id": "...", "action": "send", "target": "natter:p0412",
 "args": {"kind": "text"}, "text": "how was the climb?"}
```

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ArgSpec:
    kind: str                          # "enum" | "text" | "int" | "id" | "list"
    values: tuple[str, ...] = ()       # static enum values (dynamic ones come from IntentVerb.extra)
    max_len: int = 0                   # text: characters; list: items
    min: int = 0
    max: int = 0
    required: bool = False
    item: Optional["ArgSpec"] = None   # list items

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VerbRow:
    action: str
    legal: Callable[[GameState], Optional[IntentVerb]]   # None = not legal now
    payload: dict[str, ArgSpec]
    skill: str
    takes_text: bool = False
    text_cap: str = ""                 # config key for the text cap ("social.max_text_chars") or ""
    label: Callable[[GameState, dict], str] = ...  # the player's sentence, for run_guarded's `action`

def phone_verb(action: str, *, payload: dict[str, ArgSpec], skill: str,
               takes_text: bool = False, text_cap: str = "",
               label: Optional[Callable[[GameState, dict[str, Any]], str]] = None
               ) -> Callable[[Callable[[GameState], Optional[IntentVerb]]], Callable[[GameState], Optional[IntentVerb]]]
def legal_phone_intents(state: GameState) -> tuple[IntentVerb, ...]
def normalise_phone(raw: Any) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]      # {"action", "target", "args", "text"} or None
def check_payload(row: VerbRow, verb: IntentVerb, intent: dict[str, Any]) -> str   # "" or the refusal reason
def phone_refusal(intent: dict[str, Any], reason: str) -> dict[str, Any]
PHONE_SKILL_FOR_ACTION: dict[str, str]                        # filled by phone_verb registrations
```

### Execution (`engine/agents/tool_dispatcher.py`, T6)

```python
def execute_phone_intent(intent: Any, engine: GameEngine) -> dict[str, Any]   # ONE receipt, never []
```

Each phone skill (`engine/skills/builtin/social.py`) has the signature `social_<action>(target: str = "", args: Optional[dict] = None, text: str = "") -> str` and returns `json.dumps(result)` where `result` is the owning module's `dict` (`{"ok": bool, "error"?: str, ...}`). `REFUSAL_KEY` for phone results is `"error"`: a result with `ok: False` and `error` becomes a refusal receipt.

```python
def run_guarded(session, action, intent, *, authored=None, emit_callback=None, present=None, cancel=None,
                runner: Optional[Callable[..., dict[str, Any]]] = None,
                admit: bool = True, metric: str = "turn") -> tuple[Optional[dict], str, bool]
```

```python
# engine/social/turn.py
def run_mechanics(session: GameSession, action: str, intent: Any = None, *,
                  authored: Any = None, emit_callback: Optional[Callable[[str, dict], None]] = None) -> dict[str, Any]
```

### Session additions (`engine/session/store.py`)

`GameSession` gains (T10): `epoch: int = 0` (set in `_build` from `itertools.count(1)` under `_guard`), `dirty: bool = False`, `store: Optional[SessionStore] = None` (the store that built it, set by `_build` to `self`; Plan decision 28). (T14): `voicing: dict[str, str]` (message id -> pass id), `typing: set[str]` (thread ids), `inbox: SessionInbox`. None of these is ever saved.

`session.typing` and `session.voicing` are mutated by pass threads holding no turn lock (pre-flight F22): every reader iterates a copy -- `tuple(session.typing)`, `tuple(session.voicing.items())` -- never the live container. No per-session lock is added for them.

`engine/session/store.py::release_turn(session) -> None` (T10, Plan decision 31): `session.lock.release()`, then `session.end_after_turn()` if set. The ONE way a turn lock is let go; re-exported as `engine.social.saves.release_turn`.

### Liveness (`engine/social/saves.py`, T10; used by T14, T18, T19)

```python
def alive(session: GameSession, epoch: int, store: Optional[SessionStore] = None) -> bool:
    """The generation check (spec §16.2), against the session's OWN store (Plan decision 28)."""
    store = store or getattr(session, "store", None) or _default_store()
    return (store.get(session.session_id, session.owner) is session
            and session.epoch == epoch and not session.ending)


def keeps_nothing(session: GameSession) -> bool:
    """A harness save store (`keeps_nothing = True`, T15): no timer, no write, no thread."""
    return bool(getattr(session.saves, "keeps_nothing", False))
```

### Payload (`engine/social/payload.py`)

`phone_block(state: GameState, session: Optional[GameSession] = None, *, receipt: Optional[dict] = None) -> dict[str, Any]`; `section(name: str, *, time_varying: bool = False)` decorator registering `fn(state, session) -> Any` under key `name`. `TIME_VARYING_SECTIONS: set[str]` holds the keys whose contents may move while in-game time passes with the player away (`now` and `apps` from T6; every section registered `time_varying=True` joins it: `deck`, `likes_you` (T7), `threads`, `notifications` (T8), `requests` (T9), `games` (T22), `calendar` (T24), `contacts` (T29)); T16's 72-hour test reads it (pre-flight F14). `building(session, receipt)` is a context manager setting a `ContextVar` that `phone_block` reads when called with neither, so `GameState.to_client_dict` -- which adds `"phone": phone_block(self)` only when `social.declared()` -- builds the block ONCE with the session (typing) and the receipt (refusal) inside `run_mechanics` (Plan decision 32). Every push (`phone_message`, `media_ready`) builds its rows with the session passed explicitly.

### Voicing (`engine/social/voicing.py`, T13; consumed by T14, T15, T24)

```python
@dataclass
class PersonSnapshot:
    pid: str
    thread_id: str
    profile: dict[str, Any]          # population.profile(...) as a dict
    dynamic: dict[str, Any]          # deep copy of people[pid], rev included
    tail: list[dict[str, Any]]       # the thread's last social.memory.verbatim messages
    summary: str
    due: list[dict[str, Any]]        # the due message rows (decision included), oldest first
    proposals: tuple[str, ...]       # §10.5 rows legal at snapshot
    player: dict[str, Any]           # the player's profile as this person saw it
    now: dict[str, Any]              # {day, hour, minute, weekday, status}
    contacts: tuple[str, ...]        # display names of everyone else with a thread (agreement: another person)
    seed: int                        # state.rng_seed: the fallback's stable_rng seed (pre-flight F9)

@dataclass
class VoicedReply:
    pid: str
    thread_id: str
    message_ids: tuple[str, ...]
    bubbles: list[str]
    caption: str
    regard: dict[str, Any]           # {"warmth": int, "trust": int, "mood": str}
    learned: list[str]
    shared: list[str]
    proposals: list[str]
    fallback: bool
    failures: list[str]              # agreement failures recorded in receipts

def voice(snap: PersonSnapshot, llm_fn: Optional[LLMFn]) -> VoicedReply
def fallback_reply(snap: PersonSnapshot, why: str, *, failures: Sequence[str] = ()) -> VoicedReply   # failures kept in the reply (F9)
```

`LLMFn = Callable[[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, Any]], str]` -- messages and the `response_format`, returning raw text. Production binds it to `get_backend().chat(messages, profile="texter", response_format=fmt, reasoning="off", label="social_voice").content`.

### Thread summaries (`engine/social/memory.py`, T12; consumed by T14)

The summarizer's model function takes ONE argument, unlike the voicer's `LLMFn` (pre-flight F10):

```python
SummaryFn = Callable[[list[dict[str, Any]]], str]                     # messages -> raw text
def summarize_thread(view: dict[str, Any], llm_fn: Optional[SummaryFn]) -> tuple[str, bool]
def production_summary_fn() -> Optional[SummaryFn]   # the `small` profile (utility lane), reasoning "off"; None when no server answers
```

T14's voicer calls `summarize_thread(view, voicer's summary fn or memory.production_summary_fn())`; tests set the summary fn with `voicer.set_summary_fn(<one-argument stub>)`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces:
  - `engine.social.declared() -> bool`
  - `engine.social.social_dir() -> Optional[Path]`
  - `engine.social.load_file(directory: Path, name: str) -> dict[str, Any]` (pure; `ValueError` naming file on a parse fault)
  - `engine.social.data(name: str) -> dict[str, Any]` (cached for the active story; `{}` for a missing optional file)
  - `engine.social.load_all() -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]` (the WARMERS loader)
  - `engine.social.setting(key: str, default: Any) -> Any` (reads `social.<key>` from config)
  - `engine.social.boot() -> None` (imports the submodules; each later task appends one line)
  - `engine.social.kinds.SOCIAL_EFFECT_KINDS`, `empty_slice()`
  - `engine.game.rng.SOCIAL_*` (seven constants)
  - `GameState.social: dict[str, Any]`
  - `GameManifest.endless: bool`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** -- `tests/test_social_seam.py`:

```python
"""
The `paths.social` seam (v0.22.0 spec §2.1-2.4).

A story that declares no `paths.social` pays nothing: a turn of it adds no
engine.social submodule to sys.modules (and a fresh interpreter imports none),
its payload carries no `phone` key, and that payload is byte-identical to the
one v0.21.1 built (recorded before T1 changed any engine file).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

SIX = ["clockwork-dark", "wicked-garden", "neon-city", "the-long-con", "dev-story", "hue-and-cry"]

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PAYLOADS = Path(__file__).parent / "fixtures" / "social_seam_payloads_v0211.json"

#: The one submodule every story imports: data-only names, read by effects.py (Plan decision 2).
ALWAYS = {"engine.social.kinds"}


def _social_names(names) -> set[str]:
    return {n for n in names if n.startswith("engine.social.") and n not in ALWAYS}


def _payload(slug: str, volatile: tuple[str, ...] = ("session_id",)) -> str:
    """One story's payload after a 3-hour advance at seed 99, minus its volatile keys, as sorted JSON."""
    from engine.game import clock, procgen
    from engine.games import registry

    registry.activate(slug)
    try:
        state = procgen.new_game_state(player_name="Ash", seed=99)
        clock.advance_time(state, 3.0)
        body = state.to_client_dict()
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()
    for key in volatile:
        body.pop(key, None)
    return json.dumps(body, sort_keys=True)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("slug", SIX)
def test_a_story_without_paths_social_carries_no_phone_and_imports_nothing(slug: str) -> None:
    """A SNAPSHOT of sys.modules, never a pop: popping builds second registries (pre-flight F8)."""
    from engine import social
    from engine.game import clock, procgen
    from engine.games import registry

    before = set(sys.modules)
    registry.activate(slug)
    try:
        assert social.declared() is False
        state = procgen.new_game_state(player_name="Ash", seed=1234)
        clock.advance_time(state, 3.0)
        payload = state.to_client_dict()
        assert "phone" not in payload
        assert state.social == {}
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()
    added = sorted(_social_names(set(sys.modules) - before))
    assert added == [], added


@pytest.mark.parametrize("slug", SIX)
def test_the_payload_is_byte_identical_to_v0211(slug: str) -> None:
    recorded = json.loads(PAYLOADS.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert _payload(slug, tuple(recorded["volatile"])) == recorded["payloads"][slug]


_CHILD = """
import json, sys
from engine.game import clock, procgen
from engine.games import registry
for slug in sys.argv[1:]:
    registry.activate(slug)
    try:
        state = procgen.new_game_state(player_name="Ash", seed=1234)
        clock.advance_time(state, 3.0)
        assert "phone" not in state.to_client_dict(), slug
    finally:
        registry.deactivate()
print(json.dumps(sorted(n for n in sys.modules if n.startswith("engine.social.") and n != "engine.social.kinds")))
"""


@pytest.mark.process
def test_a_fresh_interpreter_imports_no_social_submodule_for_any_shipped_story() -> None:
    """The honest "never imported" (F8): one child interpreter plays a turn of all six stories."""
    import subprocess

    done = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", _CHILD, *SIX], cwd=str(ROOT),
                          capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=240)
    assert done.returncode == 0, done.stderr[-2000:]
    assert json.loads(done.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1]) == []


def test_every_social_kind_is_engine_only() -> None:
    from engine.game.effects import ENGINE_ONLY_EFFECTS
    from engine.social.kinds import SOCIAL_EFFECT_KINDS

    assert SOCIAL_EFFECT_KINDS <= ENGINE_ONLY_EFFECTS
    assert {"custody_served", "law_deed_committed"} <= ENGINE_ONLY_EFFECTS
    assert all(kind.startswith("social_") for kind in SOCIAL_EFFECT_KINDS)


def test_the_social_streams_are_distinct_and_new() -> None:
    from engine.game import rng

    names = [rng.SOCIAL_POPULATION, rng.SOCIAL_MATCH, rng.SOCIAL_PACE, rng.SOCIAL_BEHAVIOUR,
             rng.SOCIAL_MEDIA, rng.SOCIAL_MEET, rng.SOCIAL_GAME]
    assert len(set(names)) == 7
    others = {v for k, v in vars(rng).items() if k.isupper() and isinstance(v, str) and not k.startswith("SOCIAL_")}
    assert not set(names) & others


def test_state_social_round_trips_and_an_old_save_loads_empty() -> None:
    from engine.game.state import GameState

    state = GameState()
    state.social = {"player": {"name": "Sam"}, "seq": 3}
    again = GameState.from_dict(state.to_save_dict())
    assert again.social == state.social
    old = state.to_save_dict()
    old.pop("social")
    assert GameState.from_dict(old).social == {}


def test_an_endless_manifest_may_not_declare_endings(tmp_path) -> None:
    from engine.games.manifest import from_dict
    from engine.games.validation import StoryValidator

    manifest = from_dict(
        {"id": "endless-probe", "title": "P", "version": "0.1.0", "engine_requires": ">=0.2.0",  # Plan decision 23
         "endless": True, "paths": {"endings": "x.yaml"}},
        slug="endless-probe",
        root=tmp_path,
    )
    issues = StoryValidator(manifest).run()
    assert any(i.ref_id == "endless" and i.severity == "error" for i in issues), issues
```

```python
"""
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `engine/social/__init__.py`**:

```python
"""
The social engine (v0.22.0, docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-08-v0.22.0-dating-sim-design.md).

Declared by ONE manifest key, `paths.social`, a directory whose files are
found by fixed name (AUTHORING §3.15). This module is the only part of the
package any other engine module imports unconditionally: it reads config and
nothing else. `boot()` imports the submodules, and is called only once the
story is known to declare the key.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import threading
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Optional

from engine import yamlio
from engine.config import get_config

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

#: Every fixed file name the directory may hold (spec §2.1).
FILES: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "creation", "population", "behaviours", "apps", "relationships", "media", "venues", "games",
)

#: name -> parsed YAML, for the ACTIVE story. NULLED_ATTRIBUTES row; read once
#: into a local (tests/test_cache_reset_race.py).
_DATA_CACHE: Optional[dict[str, dict[str, Any]]] = None
_DATA_LOCK = threading.Lock()


def social_dir() -> Optional[Path]:
    rel = str(get_config().get("paths.social", "") or "").strip()
    return (_ROOT / rel) if rel else None


def declared() -> bool:
    """Whether the running story declares `paths.social`."""
    return social_dir() is not None


def load_file(directory: Path, name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """One `<name>.yaml` from `directory`; {} when absent. A parse fault is a ValueError naming it."""
    path = directory / f"{name}.yaml"
    if not path.is_file():
        return {}
    try:
        loaded = yamlio.safe_load(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 -- re-raised as the load rule's ValueError
        raise ValueError(f"social: {path.name}: does not parse: {exc}") from None
    if loaded is None:
        return {}
    if not isinstance(loaded, dict):
        raise ValueError(f"social: {path.name}: the top level must be a mapping")
    return loaded


def load_all() -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Every file of the active story's social directory (the WARMERS loader)."""
    global _DATA_CACHE
    cached = _DATA_CACHE
    if cached is not None:
        return cached
    with _DATA_LOCK:
        if _DATA_CACHE is None:
            directory = social_dir()
            _DATA_CACHE = {name: load_file(directory, name) for name in FILES} if directory else {}
        cached = _DATA_CACHE
    return cached


def data(name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return load_all().get(name, {})


def setting(key: str, default: Any) -> Any:
    """`social.<key>` from config (engine bounds, spec §20)."""
    return get_config().get(f"social.{key}", default)


def boot() -> None:
    """Import every submodule, registering its effect kinds, verbs and payload sections."""
    # One line per task, in task order (Plan decision 12):
    # from engine.social import population  # noqa: F401  (T3)
```

`engine.yamlio.safe_load` is the libyaml-backed loader (v0.21.1); never `yaml.load`. `_DATA_LOCK` is a module lock built at import: put it in `engine/locks.py`'s ORDER immediately before `"engine.net._lock"` (it is taken holding nothing but possibly `_warm_lock`, and under it only the config's lock is taken, by `social_dir()`), with one sentence in the module docstring saying so; add it to `tests/lock_order.py::ORDER` at the same place; and at the module's bottom `renew_after_fork(globals(), _DATA_LOCK=threading.Lock)` (`from engine.locks import renew_after_fork`, the `engine/config.py:88` pattern).

and in `to_client_dict` spread `**self._phone_block(),` after `**self._job_block(),`:

```python
    def _phone_block(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        The phone (v0.22.0): only for a story declaring `paths.social`, so
        every other story's payload stays byte-identical. Never raises.
        """
        try:
            from engine import social

            if not social.declared():
                return {}
            social.boot()
            from engine.social import payload
        except ImportError:
            # T2-T5 only: a declared story before T6 has no payload module yet.
            # Quiet, not an error (pre-flight F25); T6 deletes this branch.
            logger.debug("[state] No phone payload module yet (operation=_phone_block)")
            return {}
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 -- a broken phone file costs the screen, not the turn
            logger.exception("[state] Phone block failed (operation=_phone_block)")
            return {}
        try:
            return {"phone": payload.phone_block(self)}
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 -- a broken phone file costs the screen, not the turn
            logger.exception("[state] Phone block failed (operation=_phone_block)")
            return {}
```

(`engine.social.payload` arrives in T6; until then the `from engine.social import payload` line raises `ImportError`, logged at DEBUG -- T4's HTTP test reaches it, and it must not log an exception per payload (F25). T6 deletes the `except ImportError` branch: once the module exists, an ImportError is a fault and goes to the broad catch at ERROR. Write the block now so T6 only fills the module.)

```python
    def check_endless(self) -> None:
        """`endless: true` refuses `paths.endings` and `paths.epilogues` (spec §2.4)."""
        raw = self.manifest.extras.get("endless")
        if raw is not None and not isinstance(raw, bool):
            self._add("game.yaml", "endless", "must be true or false")
        if raw is True:
            for key in ("endings", "epilogues"):
                if self.manifest.paths.get(key):
                    self._add("game.yaml", "endless", f"an endless story may not declare paths.{key}")

    def check_social(self) -> None:
        """`paths.social` (spec §2.1): each file parses; each data task adds its own checks."""
        directory = self._dir("social")
        if directory is None:
            return
        from engine import social

        for name in social.FILES:
            try:
                social.load_file(directory, name)
            except ValueError as exc:
                self._add(f"social/{name}.yaml", name, str(exc))
```

```python
#: S11 (v0.22.0, spec §19.6): READ RECEIPTS joins the catalogue. One row is
#: added where the catalogue's order puts it, and nothing else moves.
READ_RECEIPTS_ROW: dict[str, Any] = {
    # Written by hand from games/read-receipts/game.yaml in T2 (engine_requires
    # ">=0.2.0", Plan decision 23: ">=0.22.0" would make the row unplayable) -- every key the
    # other rows carry (read a row of the recorded games.json for the full
    # set: active, archetypes, blurb, engine_requires, entry_location, id,
    # paths, playable, slug, title, ui, ui_plugin, version, ...), with this
    # story's values. Reviewed against a live /api/games capture once, by hand.
}


def with_read_receipts(value: Any) -> None:
    body = value["json"]
    rows = body["games"]
    assert all(row.get("slug") != "read-receipts" for row in rows), "S11 applied twice"
    rows.append(dict(READ_RECEIPTS_ROW))
    rows.sort(key=catalog_key)  # engine.games.registry.catalog_key: the route's own sort key
    assert len(rows) == 7


RECEIPTS_SANCTION: tuple[str, Callable[[str], str]] = ("games.json", json_sanction(with_read_receipts))
```

Prompt files (rule 12: these are the story's register, written by the story; the engine adds nothing):

- `prompts/texter.md`: a persona section ("You are writing text messages as one person on a phone...", the first-person-in-their-voice rules, length habits from texting style), then an `## Instructions` table whose rows are the situation ids of spec §11.3 (`opener`, `reply`, `double_text`, `anxious_check_in`, `good_morning`, `decline_photo`, `decline_voice`, `decline_video`, `send_selfie`, `send_photo`, `send_voice`, `send_video`, `reveal_turn`, `post_meet`, `reconnect`, `game_banter`, `offer_number`, `ask_meet`, `answer_request`), one line each, and a `## Fallbacks` YAML fence keyed by archetype (`romantic`, `slow_burner`, `banterer`, `dry_texter`, `needy`, `ghoster`, `breadcrumber`, `drifter`, `turner`, `steady`, `busy`, `night_owl`) then by situation, each 3-6 short lines. Format pinned by T13's parser: an `## Instructions` heading followed by `- \`<id>\`: <line>` bullets; a `## Fallbacks` heading followed by one fenced `yaml` block. Example rows:

````markdown
## Instructions

- `opener`: You matched a few minutes ago. Write the first message. Pick one thing from their profile.
- `reply`: Answer what they last said, the way you text.
- `decline_photo`: They asked for a photo. You are not sending one right now; say so in your own way.

## Fallbacks

```yaml
dry_texter:
  reply: ["ha", "yeah fair", "same"]
  opener: ["hey"]
romantic:
  reply: ["that made me smile", "tell me more about that"]
```
````

- `prompts/thread_summary.md`: the system prompt for one thread's running summary ("Keep a running summary of one text conversation ... at most 180 words ... who said what, plans, facts each learned").
- `prompts/date_recap.md`: the system prompt for a meet-up recap ("second person, at most 120 words, the band you are given is how it went").

`data/social/*.yaml`: each a header (what the file holds, which module reads it, "Every number here is a balance constant (rule 10), unmeasured until T26") and `{}`.

### Task 3: Population (§4)

**Files:**
- Create: `engine/social/population.py`
- Create: `tests/social_fixtures.py` (`receipts_active`, `receipts_state`; Plan decision 19)
- Modify: `games/read-receipts/data/social/population.yaml` (all tables; schema below), `apps.yaml` (for `matchstick` and `front_porch`: `label`, `kind: dating`, `capabilities`, the `pool:` rows and `culture.archetype_weights/intent_weights`; T7 adds the rest -- pre-flight F6: T6's `edit_profile` row is legal only for an app whose `kind` is `dating`, so these keys must exist before T6)
- Modify: `engine/social/__init__.py` (`boot()` line), `engine/locks.py` + `tests/lock_order.py` (leaf `engine.social.population._PROFILES_LOCK`), `tests/fixtures/module_state.yaml`, `engine/games/validation.py` (`check_social` gains population checks), `engine/games/caches.py` (`RELOADERS` row `("engine.social.population", "reset_profiles")`, pre-flight F7)
- Test: `tests/test_social_population.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: T1's `data`, `SOCIAL_POPULATION`, `stable_rng`.
- Produces:
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) class Person` (fields below)
  - `person(seed: int, index: int, player: Optional[dict] = None) -> Person`
  - `profile(state: GameState, pid: str) -> Person` (cached per seed, player and filter mode)
  - `reset_profiles() -> None` (a `RELOADERS` row: a data reload or a filter change never serves stale people)
  - `pid_for(index: int) -> str` (`f"p{index:04d}"`), `index_of(pid: str) -> int`
  - `pool(app: str) -> range` and `overlap(app: str) -> range`; `apps_of(pid) -> tuple[str, ...]`
  - `table(name: str) -> list[dict]` (creation's `of_table` source: `hobbies`, `hair`, `style`, `jobs`, `photo_set`)

`population.yaml` schema (bulk content by count; ~1800 lines of rows):

```yaml
filter: {sex: opposite, age_from_discovery: true}   # opposite | same | any (§4.2)
age_spill: 0.2
names: {female: [...], male: [...]}                 # >= 150 each
ages: {matchstick: {mean: 29, sd: 6}, front_porch: {mean: 33, sd: 7}}
neighbourhoods:                                     # exactly 11
  - {id: harbourside, label: Harbourside}
distance: {harbourside: {harbourside: 0, old_town: 1, ...}, ...}   # 11x11 symmetric, 0-4
jobs:                                               # >= 40
  - {id: nurse, label: Nurse, shape: shifts, education: [degree]}
schedules: {office: {...}, shifts: {...}, nights: {...}, student: {...}, freelance: {...}}  # hour bands per weekday
hobbies:                                            # >= 40; tags used by Jaccard
  - {id: climbing, label: Climbing, tags: [outdoors, active]}
traits: [{id: dry_humour, label: Dry humour, weight: 3}, ...]   # >= 30
hair: [...]; style: [...]; build: [...]; height: [...]          # creation's attribute tables
appearance: {eyes: [...], skin: [...], features: [...]}
flags: {has_pet: 0.3, has_kids: 0.15, lives_with_flatmates: 0.35}
intents: {matchstick: {short_term: 4, unsure: 3, long_term: 2, friendship: 1}, front_porch: {...}}
texting_styles:                                     # >= 8
  - {id: lowercase_fast, length: short, caps: lower, punctuation: light, emoji: 0.2, questions: 0.3, humour: playful}
bios: {lowercase_fast: ["{hobby_a} > everything", ...], ...}     # templated by style; {hobby_a}, {job}, {neighbourhood}
prompts: {front_porch: [{q: "A perfect Sunday", a_by_hobby: {climbing: ["..."]}}]}
photo_set:                                          # >= 30; also the player's
  - {id: hike_squint, label: "on a hike, squinting at the camera", scene: "mountain trail, midday light"}
preferences: {selectivity: {mean: 0.45, sd: 0.2}, dealbreakers: [smokes_yes, kids_dont_want, ...]}
```

```python
"""A person is a pure function of (seed, index) (spec §4, §19.1)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from tests.social_fixtures import receipts_active  # noqa: F401 -- created in this task; see Step 3


def test_a_person_is_the_same_whatever_order_anyone_was_met_in(receipts_active) -> None:
    from engine.social import population

    a = [population.person(7, i) for i in (5, 400, 12)]
    population.reset_profiles()
    b = [population.person(7, i) for i in (12, 5, 400)]
    assert {p.pid: p for p in a} == {p.pid: p for p in b}


def test_no_draw_uses_a_salted_hash_or_bare_random(receipts_active) -> None:
    """Cross-process stability (spec §4.1): every draw is stable_rng (blake2b), never hash() or random.*."""
    import ast
    import inspect

    from engine.social import population

    tree = ast.parse(inspect.getsource(population))
    called = {n.func.id for n in ast.walk(tree) if isinstance(n, ast.Call) and isinstance(n.func, ast.Name)}
    assert "hash" not in called
    imported = {a.name for n in ast.walk(tree) if isinstance(n, (ast.Import, ast.ImportFrom)) for a in n.names}
    assert "random" not in imported


@pytest.mark.parametrize("sex, mode, expected", [
    ("female", "opposite", {"male"}), ("male", "opposite", {"female"}),
    ("female", "same", {"female"}), ("male", "any", {"male", "female"}),
])
def test_the_filter(receipts_active, monkeypatch, sex, mode, expected) -> None:
    from engine.social import population

    monkeypatch.setitem(population._filter_spec(), "sex", mode)
    player = {"sex": sex, "discovery": {"age_min": 25, "age_max": 38}}
    seen = {population.person(3, i, player).sex for i in range(200)}
    assert seen == expected


def test_changing_the_filter_reshuffles_nothing_else(receipts_active, monkeypatch) -> None:
    from engine.social import population

    player = {"sex": "female", "discovery": {"age_min": 25, "age_max": 38}}
    before = population.person(3, 17, player)
    monkeypatch.setitem(population._filter_spec(), "sex", "same")
    population.reset_profiles()
    after = population.person(3, 17, player)
    assert before.hobbies == after.hobbies and before.archetype == after.archetype
    assert before.personality == after.personality and before.neighbourhood == after.neighbourhood


def test_pools_are_disjoint_but_for_the_overlap_band(receipts_active) -> None:
    from engine.social import population

    match, porch = population.pool("matchstick"), population.pool("front_porch")
    shared = set(match) & set(porch)
    assert len(shared) == 0
    band = population.overlap("matchstick")
    assert len(band) == 60
    assert all(set(population.apps_of(population.pid_for(i))) == {"matchstick", "front_porch"} for i in band)


def test_ages_respect_discovery_widened_by_the_spill(receipts_active) -> None:
    from engine.social import population

    player = {"sex": "female", "discovery": {"age_min": 25, "age_max": 38}}
    ages = [population.person(9, i, player).age for i in range(300)]
    assert min(ages) >= 18
    assert min(ages) >= int(25 * 0.8) and max(ages) <= round(38 * 1.2)


def test_profiles_cost_no_model_call(receipts_active, monkeypatch) -> None:
    from engine.llm import backend
    from engine.social import population

    monkeypatch.setattr(backend, "get_backend", lambda: pytest.fail("a profile called the model"))
    for i in range(50):
        population.person(1, i)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `engine/social/population.py`**

```python
"""
The population (v0.22.0 spec §4): lazy, seeded, never stored.

A person is a pure function of (seed, index) -- stable_rng(seed,
f"{SOCIAL_POPULATION}:{index}"), no state counter -- so whoever was met
first, the same seed always holds the same people. Only DYNAMIC state is kept
(state.social["people"][pid], T7 on). Static profiles are cached per seed in a
small LRU under a LEAF lock; a profile is generated OUTSIDE the lock (it reads
story data through cached loaders that take ordered locks), and two threads
generating the same person race harmlessly: identical results, first insert wins.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import threading
from collections import OrderedDict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Optional

from engine import social
from engine.game.rng import SOCIAL_POPULATION, stable_rng

#: (seed, index, player-key) -> Person. Bounded; per process.
_PROFILES: "OrderedDict[tuple[int, int, str], Person]" = OrderedDict()
_PROFILES_LOCK = threading.Lock()   # a LEAF: its holder takes nothing
PROFILE_CACHE_MAX = 4096


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Person:
    pid: str
    index: int
    name: str
    sex: str
    age: int
    neighbourhood: str
    job: str
    job_shape: str
    education: str
    hobbies: tuple[str, ...]
    attributes: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]          # (key, value), sorted
    flags: tuple[str, ...]                           # the fixed facts a mechanic may gate on
    appearance: str                                  # the image subject's descriptor (§13.3)
    personality: tuple[tuple[str, int], ...]         # the five sliders, 1-5
    traits: tuple[str, ...]                          # three
    intent: str                                      # friendship | short_term | long_term | unsure
    archetype: str
    texting_style: str
    preferences: tuple[tuple[str, Any], ...]         # age_min, age_max, dealbreakers, liked_hobbies, selectivity
    chemistry: float
    voice_id: str
    bio: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)      # app -> bio (deterministic, never the model's)
    prompts: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = ()              # Front Porch (question, answer)
    photos: tuple[str, ...] = ()                           # photo_set ids, 2-5

    def has_flag(self, name: str) -> bool:
        return name in self.flags


def pid_for(index: int) -> str:
    return f"p{index:04d}"


def index_of(pid: str) -> int:
    return int(pid[1:])


def _filter_spec() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return social.data("population").setdefault("filter", {"sex": "opposite", "age_from_discovery": True})


def _sex_for(player: dict[str, Any], rng) -> str:
    mode = str(_filter_spec().get("sex", "opposite"))
    mine = str(player.get("sex") or "female")
    if mode == "opposite":
        return "male" if mine == "female" else "female"
    if mode == "same":
        return mine
    if mode == "any":
        return rng.choice(("female", "male"))
    raise ValueError(f"social: population.yaml: filter.sex must be opposite, same or any, not {mode!r}")


def person(seed: int, index: int, player: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None) -> Person:
    player = player or {}
    key = (int(seed), int(index), _player_key(player))
    with _PROFILES_LOCK:
        hit = _PROFILES.get(key)
        if hit is not None:
            _PROFILES.move_to_end(key)
            return hit
    made = _generate(int(seed), int(index), player)       # OUTSIDE the lock
    with _PROFILES_LOCK:
        existing = _PROFILES.setdefault(key, made)
        _PROFILES.move_to_end(key)
        while len(_PROFILES) > PROFILE_CACHE_MAX:
            _PROFILES.popitem(last=False)
    return existing


def _player_key(player: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """Everything a person's draw depends on beyond (seed, index): the FILTER MODE included (F7)."""
    discovery = player.get("discovery") or {}
    mode = str(_filter_spec().get("sex", "opposite"))
    return f"{mode}|{player.get('sex', '')}|{discovery.get('age_min', '')}|{discovery.get('age_max', '')}"


def profile(state, pid: str) -> Person:
    return person(state.rng_seed, index_of(pid), state.social.get("player") or {})


def reset_profiles() -> None:
    with _PROFILES_LOCK:
        _PROFILES.clear()
```

`_generate` draws, in THIS FIXED ORDER from one `rng = stable_rng(seed, f"{SOCIAL_POPULATION}:{index}")`, every field EXCEPT sex first, then sex from a SECOND stream `stable_rng(seed, f"{SOCIAL_POPULATION}:{index}:sex")`, then the name from a third `...:{index}:name` keyed by that sex (so a filter change moves sex and name and nothing else -- spec §4.2's "drawn after the filter"): app (from `index`'s pool range, no draw), age (normal from `ages[app]`, clamped to discovery widened by `age_spill`, floor 18 -- data), neighbourhood, job (+ shape, education), hobbies (2-6, weighted), attributes, flags (each a Bernoulli on `flags:`), appearance (one from each `appearance` table, joined), personality (five sliders 1-5), traits (three without replacement, weighted), intent (per-app weights), archetype (per-app `culture.archetype_weights` from `apps.yaml`), texting style, preferences, chemistry (uniform in `apps.yaml` `matching.chemistry`; until T7 adds that block, `(-0.15, 0.15)` read with that default from `social.data("apps").get("matching", {})`), voice id (from `tts.voices` of that sex, T21; until then `""`), bio per app from the style's bank (templated; `str.format_map` with a dict of hobby/job/neighbourhood labels), prompts (Front Porch, 3), photos (2-5 from `photo_set`). Weighted draws use one helper `_weighted(rng, rows, key="weight")`. Write the helper and each step; keep `_generate` under ~120 lines by one helper per field group.

`pool(app)`: `range(cfg["pool"]["from"], cfg["pool"]["from"] + cfg["pool"]["size"])`, the two apps' ranges disjoint (spec §4). `overlap(app)`: a THIRD range after both pools, `range(max_end, max_end + overlap)` where `max_end` is the larger pool end and `overlap` the 60 of `apps.yaml` -- the same band whichever app asks; `apps_of(pid)` lists its people as on both apps. Matchstick's deck draws from `pool("matchstick")` plus the band, Front Porch's from its own pool plus the same band.

`table(name)`: `social.data("population").get(name) or []`; a missing table named by `creation.yaml` is a validation error (T4).

`engine/games/validation.py::check_social` gains `_check_population(directory)`: the filter enum, 11 neighbourhoods and a symmetric distance table, every name pool non-empty, every weight a positive number, every `bios` style a declared `texting_styles` id -- each a `self._add("social/population.yaml", <key>, <message>)`.

Module state rows: `engine.social.population._PROFILES: {verdict: locked}`, `engine.social.population._PROFILES_LOCK: {verdict: locked}`, `engine.social.population.PROFILE_CACHE_MAX: {verdict: constant}`. Locks: add `engine.social.population._PROFILES_LOCK` to `engine/locks.py`'s LEAF list (count and prose updated: "Fifteen per-object locks..." -- it is a module lock but a leaf; say so in one sentence) and `tests/lock_order.py::LEAVES`; `renew_after_fork(globals(), _PROFILES_LOCK=threading.Lock)` at module bottom.

`boot()` gains `from engine.social import population  # noqa: F401 (T3)`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `creation.apply`:

```python
class CreationError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, field: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"{field}: {message}")
        self.field = field


def apply(state: GameState, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Validate, fill defaults, write state.social through `social_profile` (spec §3.2)."""
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise CreationError("creation", "must be an object")
    form = social.data("creation").get("fields") or {}
    profile = _resolve(form, payload, prefix="", seed=state.rng_seed)   # raises CreationError naming the dotted field
    result = apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_profile", "profile": profile})
    if not result.get("ok"):
        raise CreationError("creation", result.get("text", "refused"))
    return state.social["player"]


@effect_kind("social_profile")
def _e_social_profile(state: GameState, effect: dict[str, Any], ctx: EffectContext) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if state.social.get("player"):
        return {"type": "social_profile", "ok": False, "text": "a profile already exists"}
    state.social = {**empty_slice(), "player": dict(effect["profile"])}
    return {"type": "social_profile", "ok": True, "text": "profile created"}
```

`_resolve(form, payload, prefix, *, seed: int)` (the seed is passed in: the seeded defaults need it, and `_resolve` takes no state): unknown keys refused (`CreationError(prefix+key, "unknown field")`); per `kind`: `text` (str, stripped, `<= max`, default), `choice` (in `of` or the table's ids), `int` (`min <= v <= max`, bools refused), `multi` (list of distinct table ids, `min <= len <= max`; default: the first `min` ids of a seeded pick -- `stable_rng(seed, "social.population:player")` so defaults are deterministic), `sliders` (dict of each slider to an int in range; default 3), nested mapping (recurse with `prefix + key + "."`). `discovery.age_min <= discovery.age_max` else `CreationError("discovery", ...)`. The `photos` default: one photo.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the queue** (`engine/social/events.py`):

```python
"""
The exact-time event queue (spec §9.1). Walked inside clock.advance_time;
inert for every story without paths.social.

state.social["queue"] is a list kept sorted by (at, seq): `at` an absolute
in-game hour (float, minute resolution), `seq` from state.social["seq"], so
equal times replay in one order. Every change goes through apply_effect
(`social_queue` to push, `social_event` to apply one), rule 3.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import bisect
import logging
from typing import Any, Callable, Optional

from engine import social
from engine.game.effects import EffectContext, apply_effect, effect_kind
from engine.game.state import GameState

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

KINDS = frozenset({"see_like", "read", "reply", "initiate", "double_text", "expire",
                   "likes_you", "game_move", "meet_start", "arc_beat", "daily"})

#: kind -> handler(state, event) -> dict. Filled by each module that owns a kind.
_HANDLERS: dict[str, Callable[[GameState, dict[str, Any]], dict[str, Any]]] = {}


def handles(kind: str):
    def decorator(fn):
        _HANDLERS[kind] = fn
        return fn
    return decorator


def _max_events() -> int:
    return int(social.setting("max_events_per_advance", 2000))


def push(state: GameState, at: float, pid: str, kind: str, **data: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_queue", "at": float(at), "pid": pid, "kind": kind, "data": data})


@effect_kind("social_queue")
def _e_queue(state: GameState, effect: dict[str, Any], ctx: EffectContext) -> dict[str, Any]:
    slice_ = state.social
    slice_["seq"] = int(slice_.get("seq", 0)) + 1
    event = {"at": float(effect["at"]), "seq": slice_["seq"], "pid": str(effect.get("pid", "")),
             "kind": str(effect["kind"]), **dict(effect.get("data") or {})}
    queue = slice_.setdefault("queue", [])
    keys = [(e["at"], e["seq"]) for e in queue]
    queue.insert(bisect.bisect_right(keys, (event["at"], event["seq"])), event)
    return {"type": "social_queue", "ok": True, "text": f"queued {event['kind']}", "event": event}


@effect_kind("social_event")
def _e_event(state: GameState, effect: dict[str, Any], ctx: EffectContext) -> dict[str, Any]:
    event = effect["event"]
    handler = _HANDLERS.get(event["kind"])
    if handler is None:
        logger.error("[social] No handler for queued event (operation=advance, kind=%s)", event["kind"])
        return {"type": "social_event", "ok": False, "text": f"no handler for {event['kind']}"}
    out = handler(state, event) or {}
    return {"type": "social_event", "ok": bool(out.get("ok", True)), "text": event["kind"], "result": out}


def advance(state: GameState, from_hours: float, to_hours: float) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Pop and apply every event with at <= to_hours, in (at, seq) order; bounded."""
    if not state.social:
        return []
    applied: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    limit = _max_events()
    queue = state.social.setdefault("queue", [])
    while queue and queue[0]["at"] <= to_hours:
        if len(applied) >= limit:
            logger.error("[social] Event pass stopped at social.max_events_per_advance=%d "
                         "(operation=advance, left=%d)", limit, len(queue))
            break
        event = queue.pop(0)
        applied.append(apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_event", "event": event}))
    return applied


def cancel(state: GameState, pid: str, kinds: Optional[set[str]] = None) -> int:
    """Drop pid's queued events (a ghost, an unmatch). Called from inside a social_* handler."""
    queue = state.social.get("queue", [])
    keep = [e for e in queue if not (e["pid"] == pid and (kinds is None or e["kind"] in kinds))]
    dropped = len(queue) - len(keep)
    state.social["queue"] = keep
    return dropped
```

(`queue.pop(0)` on a list is O(n); measured fine at the bound -- record it; if T26's 72 h advance exceeds 0.5 s, switch to `heapq` on `(at, seq, event)` tuples in that task with the replay test as the guard.) `daily` is registered here: it re-queues itself at the next 04:00 and calls each module's daily hook (`events.daily_hooks: list[Callable[[GameState], None]]`, appended by T7 likes reset, T9 drift/mood, T19 budgets). The first `daily` is queued by `social_profile` (T4's handler gains one `push` -- write it in this task, editing T4's handler), with `pid=""`. A new run starts at 19:00 on day 1 (spec §15.4), so that `daily` sits in every `receipts_state`'s queue from the start: every test here asserts on ITS OWN events (filtered by `kind` or `pid`), never on the whole queue (pre-flight F5).

`engine/game/clock.py` -- after `walk.finish()` and before the death check (spec §9.1; Plan decision 2; **Plan decision 27** -- the guard is narrower than the spec's snippet, pre-flight F17):

```python
    # every other world pass, before the death check. A story that declares no
```

`engine/social/__init__.py` gains `import_fault`:

```python
#: Names already reported by import_fault (a log_once row): one ERROR per process, not one per advance.
_IMPORT_FAULTS_LOGGED: set[str] = set()


def import_fault(name: str, exc: BaseException) -> None:
    """A declared story's social submodule failed to import (Plan decision 27): ERROR, once per name."""
    if name in _IMPORT_FAULTS_LOGGED:
        return
    _IMPORT_FAULTS_LOGGED.add(name)
    logger.error("[social] A social module failed to import; its pass is skipped "
                 "(operation=import_fault, module=%s): %s", name, exc)
```

```python
The phone catalogue (spec §7, §19.2): every legal action resolves through its

"""

import pytest

                   args={"field": "bio"}, text="x" * 601)

@pytest.mark.parametrize("until, hours", [("15m", 0.25), ("1h", 1.0)])
def test_wait_advances_the_clock_through_advance_time(receipts_state, until, hours) -> None:

    assert receipt["success"] is True
    assert receipts_state.world_clock_hours == pytest.approx(before + hours)


def test_wait_morning_sleeps_to_eight(receipts_state) -> None:
    _act(receipts_state, "wait", args={"until": "morning"})

def test_wait_next_stops_at_the_cap_with_nothing_queued(receipts_state) -> None:

    before = receipts_state.world_clock_hours
    _act(receipts_state, "wait", args={"until": "next"})
    assert receipts_state.world_clock_hours - before == pytest.approx(8.0)


def test_edit_profile_and_receipts_write_through_effects(receipts_state) -> None:

                text="climbs, cooks, reads")["success"]

def test_a_local_phone_action_over_the_socket_answers_a_turn_update_with_the_phone(receipts_active) -> None:
    """Local mode never admits a turn at all, so this proves the socket path; T14's hosted twin proves no admission (F18)."""
    from engine.scenes.default_scene import create_app

    scene, app = create_app(testing=True)
    http = app.test_client()
    sid = http.post("/api/game/new", json={"player_name": "Sam", "seed": 3}).get_json()["session_id"]
    sock = scene.socketio.test_client(app, flask_test_client=http)
    sock.emit("join_session", {"session_id": sid})
    sock.get_received()
    sock.emit("phone_action", {"session_id": sid, "action": "wait", "target": "", "args": {"until": "15m"}})
    events = {e["name"]: e["args"][0] for e in sock.get_received()}
    assert events["turn_update"]["narration"] == ""
    assert "phone" in events["turn_update"]["state"]


def test_the_http_twin(receipts_active) -> None:

    _, app = create_app(testing=True)

    ok = http.post("/api/phone/action", json={"session_id": sid, "action": "wait", "args": {"until": "1h"}})
```

```python
    # hosted_receipts: a tests/hosted_app.py app with read-receipts active,
```
