- [ ] **Step 3: `engine/social/intents.py`** (complete):

```python
"""
The phone catalogue (v0.22.0 spec §7, rule 1).

Nobody samples this catalogue: the player presses it. It reuses
engine.game.intents.IntentVerb -- targets are checked by membership, dynamic
enums ride in `extra` -- and adds a PAYLOAD per verb, checked here: enums,
text caps (refused, never cut), ints in range, lists bounded. Rows are
registered with @phone_verb by the module that owns the mechanic (Plan
decision 12), so the catalogue grows task by task with no stub.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Callable, Optional

from engine import social
from engine.game.intents import IntentVerb
from engine.game.state import GameState

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

LegalFn = Callable[[GameState], Optional[IntentVerb]]
LabelFn = Callable[[GameState, dict[str, Any]], str]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ArgSpec:
    kind: str                          # enum | text | int | id | list
    values: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    max_len: int = 0
    min: int = 0
    max: int = 0
    required: bool = False
    item: Optional["ArgSpec"] = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VerbRow:
    action: str
    legal: LegalFn
    payload: dict[str, ArgSpec]
    skill: str
    takes_text: bool
    text_cap: str
    label: LabelFn


#: action -> row, in registration order (the order the client lists them).
_ROWS: dict[str, VerbRow] = {}
#: action -> skill name (tests/test_reachability.py, Plan decision 4).
PHONE_SKILL_FOR_ACTION: dict[str, str] = {}
#: action -> payload schema, for the client and the HTTP twin's docs.
PAYLOADS: dict[str, dict[str, ArgSpec]] = {}


def _default_label(state: GameState, intent: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    return f"{intent['action']} {intent.get('target', '')}".strip()


def phone_verb(action: str, *, payload: dict[str, ArgSpec], skill: str, takes_text: bool = False,
               text_cap: str = "", label: Optional[LabelFn] = None) -> Callable[[LegalFn], LegalFn]:
    def decorator(legal: LegalFn) -> LegalFn:
        if action in _ROWS and _ROWS[action].legal is not legal:
            raise ValueError(f"phone verb {action!r} registered twice")
        _ROWS[action] = VerbRow(action, legal, dict(payload), skill, takes_text, text_cap,
                                label or _default_label)
        PHONE_SKILL_FOR_ACTION[action] = skill
        PAYLOADS[action] = dict(payload)
        return legal
    return decorator


def row(action: str) -> Optional[VerbRow]:
    return _ROWS.get(action)


def legal_phone_intents(state: GameState) -> tuple[IntentVerb, ...]:
    """Every verb legal NOW, rebuilt from the live state (never cached)."""
    out: list[IntentVerb] = []
    for entry in _ROWS.values():
        try:
            verb = entry.legal(state)
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 -- one broken row must not empty the phone
            logger.exception("[social] Catalogue row failed (operation=legal_phone_intents, action=%s)",
                             entry.action)
            continue
        if verb is not None:
            out.append(verb)
    return tuple(out)


def normalise_phone(raw: Any) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Coerce the wire body to {action, target, args, text}; None for anything unusable."""
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        return None
    action = str(raw.get("action") or "").strip()
    if not action:
        return None
    args = raw.get("args")
    return {
        "action": action,
        "target": str(raw.get("target") or "").strip(),
        "args": dict(args) if isinstance(args, dict) else {},
        "text": str(raw.get("text") or "").strip(),
    }


def _check_arg(name: str, spec: ArgSpec, value: Any, dynamic: tuple[str, ...]) -> str:
    if value is None or value == "":
        return f"{name} is required" if spec.required else ""
    if spec.kind in ("enum", "id"):
        allowed = dynamic or spec.values
        if str(value) not in allowed:
            return f"{name} must be one of: {', '.join(allowed)}" if allowed else f"{name} is not available"
        return ""
    if spec.kind == "text":
        if not isinstance(value, str):
            return f"{name} must be text"
        if spec.max_len and len(value) > spec.max_len:
            return f"{name} is longer than {spec.max_len} characters"
        return ""
    if spec.kind == "int":
        if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int):
            return f"{name} must be a whole number"
        if not spec.min <= value <= spec.max:
            return f"{name} must be between {spec.min} and {spec.max}"
        return ""
    if spec.kind == "list":
        if not isinstance(value, list):
            return f"{name} must be a list"
        if spec.max_len and len(value) > spec.max_len:
            return f"{name} has more than {spec.max_len} items"
        if spec.item is not None:
            for i, item in enumerate(value):
                problem = _check_arg(f"{name}[{i}]", spec.item, item, ())
                if problem:
                    return problem
        return ""
    return f"{name} has an unknown kind {spec.kind!r}"


def check_payload(entry: VerbRow, verb: IntentVerb, intent: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """'' when the intent's args and text fit the row; else the refusal reason."""
    args = intent["args"]
    unknown = sorted(set(args) - set(entry.payload))
    if unknown:
        return f"{entry.action} takes no {', '.join(unknown)}"
    for name, spec in entry.payload.items():
        problem = _check_arg(name, spec, args.get(name), tuple(verb.extra.get(name, ())))
        if problem:
            return problem
    text = intent["text"]
    if text and not entry.takes_text:
        return f"{entry.action} takes no text"
    if entry.text_cap:
        cap = int(social.setting(entry.text_cap.removeprefix("social."), 600))
        if len(text) > cap:
            return f"That message is longer than {cap} characters."
    return ""


def phone_refusal(intent: dict[str, Any], reason: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """The engine declining a phone action, in a receipt's shape (rule 1)."""
    return {
        "type": "phone",
        "skill": str(intent.get("action") or "phone"),
        "args": dict(intent),
        "result": {"ok": False, "error": reason},
        "success": False,
        "refused": True,
        "intent": dict(intent),
    }
```

(The `edit_profile` text cap is `social.max_text_chars` -- 600 -- as `send`'s; `like.comment` is an `ArgSpec("text", max_len=140)`.)

- [ ] **Step 4: `execute_phone_intent`** in `engine/agents/tool_dispatcher.py`:

```python
def execute_phone_intent(intent: Any, engine: GameEngine) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Resolve one phone action (v0.22.0 spec §7.3): normalise, find the verb in
    the catalogue rebuilt NOW, check target and payload, run its system-only
    skill. ONE receipt, never []: anything illegal is a refusal in the
    engine's words, which reaches the player as a system line or a toast.
    """
    from engine.social import intents as phone

    parsed = phone.normalise_phone(intent)
    if parsed is None:
        return phone.phone_refusal({"action": "", "target": "", "args": {}, "text": ""},
                                   "That is not something the phone can do.")
    entry = phone.row(parsed["action"])
    verb = None
    if entry is not None:
        verb = intents_find(phone.legal_phone_intents(engine.state), parsed["action"])
    if entry is None or verb is None:
        return phone.phone_refusal(parsed, f"You can't {parsed['action'].replace('_', ' ')} right now.")
    if verb.targets and parsed["target"] not in verb.targets:
        return phone.phone_refusal(parsed, f"{parsed['target'] or 'That'} isn't available any more.")
    problem = phone.check_payload(entry, verb, parsed)
    if problem:
        return phone.phone_refusal(parsed, problem)
    skill_def = SKILL_REGISTRY.get(entry.skill)
    if skill_def is None or not skill_def.callable_by(AGENT_SYSTEM):
        return phone.phone_refusal(parsed, "the phone has no way to do that.")
    receipt = execute_tool(entry.skill, {"target": parsed["target"], "args": parsed["args"],
                                         "text": parsed["text"]}, engine)
    receipt["intent"] = dict(parsed)
    result = receipt.get("result")
    if isinstance(result, dict) and result.get("ok") is False:
        receipt["success"] = False
        receipt["refused"] = True
    return receipt
```

(`intents_find` is `engine.game.intents.find_verb`, imported at the top as the module already imports `intents`; `AGENT_SYSTEM` from `engine.skills.registry`. Read `execute_tool`'s handling of a JSON-string result: it parses it into `receipt["result"]`; if it does not, `json.loads` here.)

- [ ] **Step 5: The skills** -- `engine/skills/builtin/social.py`:

```python
"""
The phone's skills (v0.22.0 spec §7.3). System-only (Plan decision 4): no
storyteller or MCP agent can call them; the phone catalogue is their one
caller (tool_dispatcher.execute_phone_intent). Each is a thin shell over the
owning engine.social module, imported lazily so a story without paths.social
never loads it (spec §2.1).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from typing import Any, Optional

from engine.game.engine import get_active_engine
from engine.skills.registry import AGENT_SYSTEM, TRIGGER_SYSTEM, skill

PACK = "social"
CATEGORY = "PHONE"


def _phone_skill(description: str):
    return skill(pack=PACK, description=description, category=CATEGORY,
                 trigger=TRIGGER_SYSTEM, agents=[AGENT_SYSTEM])


@_phone_skill("Let in-game time pass on the phone.")
def social_wait(target: str = "", args: Optional[dict] = None, text: str = "") -> str:
    from engine.social import turn

    return json.dumps(turn.wait(get_active_engine().state, args or {}))


@_phone_skill("Edit one of the player's profile fields on an app.")
def social_edit_profile(target: str = "", args: Optional[dict] = None, text: str = "") -> str:
    from engine.social import turn

    return json.dumps(turn.edit_profile(get_active_engine().state, target, args or {}, text))


@_phone_skill("Turn the player's read receipts on or off.")
def social_set_receipts(target: str = "", args: Optional[dict] = None, text: str = "") -> str:
    from engine.social import turn

    return json.dumps(turn.set_receipts(get_active_engine().state, target, args or {}))
```

Each later task appends its skills here the same way (one function per action). `engine/skills/builtin/__init__.py` and `tool_dispatcher.py`'s imports gain `social`.

- [ ] **Step 6: `engine/social/turn.py`** -- `run_mechanics` (§16.1; T14 adds step 1, T19 step 3) and the three actions:

```python
"""
The phone's mechanics action (v0.22.0 spec §16.1): no model, no admission,
the turn lock held briefly by run_guarded. Steps, each added by the task
that builds it (no stub): 1 drain the inbox (T14); 2 execute; 3 create media
jobs (T19); 4 mark dirty and stamp (T10); 5 emit.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import time
from typing import Any, Callable, Optional

from engine import social
from engine.game import clock
from engine.game.effects import EffectContext, apply_effect, effect_kind
from engine.game.intents import IntentVerb
from engine.game.state import GameState
from engine.social.intents import ArgSpec, phone_verb

WAIT_UNTIL = ("next", "15m", "1h", "morning")
#: Event kinds that notify the player, which `wait {until: next}` stops at (§15.1).
NOTIFYING = frozenset({"reply", "initiate", "double_text", "see_like", "likes_you", "game_move", "meet_start"})


def played_on_the_phone() -> str:
    """
    What `player_choice` is answered with in a phone story (spec §7.2), built
    from the ACTIVE manifest's title so this engine module names no story
    (Plan decision 26). READ RECEIPTS: "Read Receipts is played on the phone."
    """
    from engine.games import registry

    manifest = registry.peek()
    title = manifest.title if manifest is not None else "This story"
    return f"{title} is played on the phone."


def run_mechanics(session: Any, action: str, intent: Any = None, *, authored: Any = None,
                  emit_callback: Optional[Callable[[str, dict[str, Any]], None]] = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from engine.agents.tool_dispatcher import execute_phone_intent
    from engine.social import payload

    state = session.engine.state
    # 2. Execute (a receipt or a refusal; costs and waits through advance_time).
    receipt = execute_phone_intent(intent, session.engine)
    # The phone block is built ONCE, inside to_client_dict, with this session's
    # typing marks and this receipt's refusal (Plan decision 32).
    with payload.building(session, receipt):
        client_state = state.to_client_dict()
    turn_payload: dict[str, Any] = {
        "narration": "",
        "choices": [],
        "receipts": [receipt],
        "state": client_state,
    }
    # 5. Emit.
    if emit_callback is not None:
        emit_callback("turn_update", turn_payload)
    return turn_payload


def _cost(state: GameState, app: str, what: str) -> float:
    """Minutes an action costs on an app (apps.yaml cost_minutes), as hours."""
    apps = social.data("apps").get("apps") or {}
    return float((apps.get(app) or {}).get("cost_minutes", {}).get(what, 0.0)) / 60.0


def _next_notifying_at(state: GameState) -> Optional[float]:
    for event in state.social.get("queue", []):
        if event["kind"] in NOTIFYING:
            return float(event["at"])
    return None


def wait(state: GameState, args: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    until = str(args.get("until") or "15m")
    now = state.world_clock_hours
    cap = float(social.setting("wait_next_cap_hours", 8))
    if until == "15m":
        hours = 0.25
    elif until == "1h":
        hours = 1.0
    elif until == "morning":
        hour = now % 24
        hours = (8 - hour) % 24 or 24.0
    else:
        due = _next_notifying_at(state)
        hours = cap if due is None else min(cap, max(0.0, due - now))
    clock.advance_time(state, hours)
    return {"ok": True, "waited_hours": hours}


@phone_verb("wait", payload={"until": ArgSpec("enum", WAIT_UNTIL, required=True)}, skill="social_wait",
            label=lambda s, i: "Put the phone down")
def _wait_verb(state: GameState) -> Optional[IntentVerb]:
    return IntentVerb("wait", extra={"until": WAIT_UNTIL})


def edit_profile(state: GameState, app: str, args: dict[str, Any], text: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    clock.advance_time(state, _cost(state, "settings", "edit"))
    return apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_settings", "field": "bio", "app": app, "value": text})


def set_receipts(state: GameState, app: str, args: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_settings", "field": "receipts", "value": args.get("to") == "on"})


def _dating_apps() -> tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]:
    apps = social.data("apps").get("apps") or {}
    return tuple((k, str(v.get("label", k))) for k, v in apps.items() if v.get("kind") == "dating")


@phone_verb("edit_profile", payload={"field": ArgSpec("enum", ("bio",), required=True)},
            skill="social_edit_profile", takes_text=True, text_cap="social.max_text_chars",
            label=lambda s, i: "Edit your profile")
def _edit_profile_verb(state: GameState) -> Optional[IntentVerb]:
    options = _dating_apps()
    return IntentVerb("edit_profile", options=options) if options else None


@phone_verb("set_receipts", payload={"to": ArgSpec("enum", ("on", "off"), required=True)},
            skill="social_set_receipts", label=lambda s, i: "Read receipts")
def _set_receipts_verb(state: GameState) -> Optional[IntentVerb]:
    return IntentVerb("set_receipts", options=(("natter", "Natter"),))


@effect_kind("social_settings")
def _e_settings(state: GameState, effect: dict[str, Any], ctx: EffectContext) -> dict[str, Any]:
    field = effect.get("field")
    if field == "bio":
        state.social["player"].setdefault("bios", {})[str(effect["app"])] = str(effect["value"])
    elif field == "receipts":
        state.social["settings"]["receipts"] = bool(effect["value"])
    else:
        return {"type": "social_settings", "ok": False, "text": f"unknown setting {field!r}"}
    return {"type": "social_settings", "ok": True, "text": f"{field} updated"}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: `engine/social/payload.py`** (core; later tasks add sections):

```python
"""
The `phone` block of the payload (v0.22.0 spec §17.3). Sections are
registered by the module that owns them (Plan decision 12). Hidden values
(warmth, interest, trust, mood, ghosted, arc, rev) never leave the server:
tests/test_ui_contract.py's key scan covers this block and both pushes.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import logging
from contextvars import ContextVar
from typing import Any, Callable, Iterator, Optional

from engine import social
from engine.game.state import GameState

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

SectionFn = Callable[[GameState, Any], Any]
_SECTIONS: dict[str, SectionFn] = {}

#: Keys whose contents may move while in-game time passes with the player away
#: (pre-flight F14): T16's 72-hour test allows exactly these to change. A
#: section joins by registering with time_varying=True.
TIME_VARYING_SECTIONS: set[str] = {"now", "apps"}

#: (session, receipt) for the payload being built by run_mechanics (Plan
#: decision 32): read by phone_block when it is called with neither.
_BUILDING: ContextVar[Optional[tuple[Any, Optional[dict[str, Any]]]]] = ContextVar("phone_building", default=None)


def section(name: str, *, time_varying: bool = False) -> Callable[[SectionFn], SectionFn]:
    def decorator(fn: SectionFn) -> SectionFn:
        _SECTIONS[name] = fn
        if time_varying:
            TIME_VARYING_SECTIONS.add(name)
        return fn
    return decorator


@contextlib.contextmanager
def building(session: Any, receipt: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None) -> Iterator[None]:
    """Build the payload's phone block with this session and receipt (to_client_dict calls phone_block(state))."""
    token = _BUILDING.set((session, receipt))
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        _BUILDING.reset(token)


WEEKDAYS = ("Mon", "Tue", "Wed", "Thu", "Fri", "Sat", "Sun")


def now_block(state: GameState) -> dict[str, Any]:
    total = state.world_clock_hours
    minutes = int(round((total % 1.0) * 60)) % 60
    return {"day": state.world_day, "hour": state.world_hour, "minute": minutes,
            "weekday": WEEKDAYS[(state.world_day - 1) % 7]}


def phone_block(state: GameState, session: Any = None, *, receipt: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from engine.config import hosting_enabled

    if session is None and receipt is None:
        context = _BUILDING.get()
        if context is not None:
            session, receipt = context

    apps = social.data("apps").get("apps") or {}
    block: dict[str, Any] = {
        "now": now_block(state),
        "auto_wait": (not hosting_enabled()) and bool(state.social.get("settings", {}).get("auto_wait")),
        "apps": [{"id": k, "label": str(v.get("label", k.title())), "kind": str(v.get("kind", "")), "badge": 0}
                 for k, v in apps.items()],
        "player": _player(state),
        "refusal": _refusal(receipt),
    }
    for name, fn in _SECTIONS.items():
        try:
            block[name] = fn(state, session)
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 -- one section's fault costs that section
            logger.exception("[social] Payload section failed (operation=phone_block, section=%s)", name)
            block[name] = None
    return block


def _player(state: GameState) -> dict[str, Any]:
    player = state.social.get("player") or {}
    keep = ("name", "sex", "age", "looking_for", "hobbies", "attributes", "personality",
            "discovery", "photos", "bios")
    return {k: player[k] for k in keep if k in player}


def _refusal(receipt: Optional[dict[str, Any]]) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
    if not receipt or not receipt.get("refused"):
        return None
    intent = receipt.get("intent") or {}
    return {"action": intent.get("action", ""), "target": intent.get("target", ""),
            "reason": str((receipt.get("result") or {}).get("error") or "That didn't work.")}
```

(`badge` counts are filled by T8's `threads` section through a `badges(state)` helper it adds; T6 ships 0. A section whose contents can move while the player is away registers `time_varying=True` -- `deck`, `likes_you` (T7), `threads`, `notifications` (T8), `requests` (T9), `games` (T22), `calendar` (T24), `contacts` (T29) -- so T16's survival test reads `TIME_VARYING_SECTIONS` instead of a hand-kept list.)

```python
#: ``phone_metric(account, event, status, ms)`` (v0.22.0): set by
#: engine.hosting.metrics_emit.install_hooks; None in local mode.
phone_metric: Optional[Callable[[str, str, str, float], None]] = None

_PHONE_STATUS = {"ok": "ok", "busy": "busy", "error": "failed"}


def _report_phone(session: Any, event: str, status: str, ms: float) -> None:
    hook = phone_metric
    if hook is None:
        return
    try:
        hook(str(getattr(session, "owner", "") or ""), event, status, float(ms))
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 -- a metric must never fail an action
        logger.warning("[default_scene] A phone metric was not recorded (operation=_report_phone)")


def _report(session: Any, metric: str, admit_ms: float, run_ms: float, outcome: str) -> None:
    if metric == "phone":
        _report_phone(session, "action", _PHONE_STATUS.get(outcome, "failed"), admit_ms + run_ms)
    else:
        _report_turn(session, admit_ms, run_ms, outcome)
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Routes** -- `engine/social/routes.py` gains (and `register` calls `_register_socket(scene)`):

```python
def _run(scene: Any, session: Any, body: dict[str, Any], emit_callback=None):
    from engine.scenes.default_scene import run_guarded
    from engine.social import intents as phone
    from engine.social.turn import run_mechanics

    parsed = phone.normalise_phone(body) or {"action": "", "target": "", "args": {}, "text": ""}
    entry = phone.row(parsed["action"])
    label = entry.label(session.engine.state, parsed) if entry else parsed["action"]
    return run_guarded(session, label, parsed, emit_callback=emit_callback,
                       runner=run_mechanics, admit=False, metric="phone")


@bp.post("/api/phone/action")       # inside blueprint(scene)
def api_phone_action() -> Any:
    body = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    try:
        session = scene.store.require(str(body.get("session_id", "")))
    except KeyError:
        return jsonify({"error": "session not found"}), 404
    turn, error, busy = _run(scene, session, body)
    if busy:
        return jsonify({"error": error, "busy": True}), 409
    if turn is None:
        return jsonify({"error": error}), 500
    return jsonify(turn)


def _register_socket(scene: Any) -> None:
    @scene.on("phone_action")
    def on_phone_action(data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        session_id = str((data or {}).get("session_id", ""))
        try:
            session = scene.store.require(session_id)
        except KeyError:
            emit("turn_error", {"message": "session not found", "fatal": True})
            return

        def _emit(event: str, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
            room_check = scene._room_check
            if room_check is not None:
                room_check(session_id)
            emit(event, payload, room=session_id)

        _, error, busy = _run(scene, session, data or {}, emit_callback=_emit)
        if error:
            emit("turn_error", {"message": error, "busy": busy})
```

(`blueprint()` becomes `blueprint(scene)` so its routes can reach `scene.store`; T4's call site is updated in this task. After `run_guarded` releases, T14 adds `voicer.request(session)` here -- spec §16.1's last paragraph.)

`engine/hosting/limits.py`:

```python
#: phone_action's text fields (v0.22.0, spec §17.1): the message or bio, and
#: every string argument (a like's comment, a voice note's transcript). Held
#: to hosting.max_input_chars like any action's text; the catalogue's own,
#: smaller caps (social.max_text_chars, 140 for a comment) apply in every mode.
def phone_over_cap(body: Any, max_input_chars: int) -> Optional[str]:
    if not isinstance(body, dict):
        return None
    limit = int(max_input_chars)
    values = [body.get("text"), body.get("action"), body.get("target")]
    args = body.get("args")
    if isinstance(args, dict):
        values.extend(v for v in args.values() if isinstance(v, str))
    for value in values:
        if value is not None and len(value if isinstance(value, str) else str(value)) > limit:
            return too_long(limit)
    return None
```

Like-back (spec §8.2, complete):

```python
def like_back_p(state: GameState, app: str, person: Person, *, super_: bool) -> float:
    m = social.data("apps")["matching"]
    culture = apps.app(app).get("culture") or {}
    p = (float(m["like_back_base"][app])
         * (float(m["compat_offset"]) + compatibility(state.social["player"], person))
         * max(0.0, 1.0 - _selectivity(person) * float(culture.get("selectivity_mult", 1.0)))
         * completeness_mult(state.social["player"]))
    if super_:
        p *= float(m["super_like_mult"])
    return max(0.0, min(1.0, p))


def likes_back(state: GameState, app: str, person: Person, *, super_: bool) -> bool:
    """Drawn ONCE per pair (spec §8.2): un-liking and liking again cannot re-roll it."""
    roll = stable_rng(state.rng_seed, f"{SOCIAL_MATCH}:{person.pid}").random()
    return roll < like_back_p(state, app, person, super_=super_)


def completeness_mult(player: dict[str, Any]) -> float:
    low, high = social.data("apps")["matching"]["completeness"]
    filled = sum(bool(player.get(k)) for k in ("photos", "hobbies", "bios", "attributes", "looking_for"))
    photos = min(1.0, len(player.get("photos") or []) / 4.0)
    share = (filled / 5.0 + photos) / 2.0
    return float(low) + (float(high) - float(low)) * share
```

`compatibility`: `weights` from `matching:`; `looking_for` from `looking_for_table` keyed `f"{a}_{b}"` in a canonical order (long/short/friendship/unsure; missing pair → 0.5); hobbies Jaccard over the union of each side's hobby `tags` from `population.yaml`; personality: per slider `1 - |a-b|/4`, except `outgoing`, which also scores 0.75 for a difference of exactly 2 (complementary allowed); preferences: age inside the person's range 1.0 else 0.0, times `dealbreaker_mult` per dealbreaker the player's attributes hit; distance: `1 - table[a][b]/4`; then `clamp(sum + person.chemistry, 0, 1)`. All deterministic, no RNG (the chemistry was drawn at generation).

  - `messaging.send(state, thread_id, kind, text, *, photo_id="", subject="", transcript="") -> dict`
  - `messaging.open_thread(state, thread_id) -> dict`
  - `messaging.thread_for(state, pid) -> Optional[str]` (the thread the person replies in: the last written-to)
  - `messaging.unvoiced(state) -> list[dict]` (every `voiced is False` message, oldest first)
  - the `threads` and `notifications` payload sections (both `time_varying=True`, pre-flight F14) and `open_thread`
  - `messaging.badges(state) -> dict[str, int]`
  - `messaging.notify(state, app, thread_id, pid, kind, preview) -> None` (bounded at `social.notifications_max`)
  - effect handlers `social_message`, `social_read`; event handlers `read`, `reply`, `initiate`, `double_text`

**`Decision` lives in `engine/social/kinds.py`** (stdlib `dataclasses` only, so the module stays import-free): T8 queues due messages carrying one, before T13's `voicing.py` exists; `voicing.py` re-exports it (`from engine.social.kinds import Decision`). Both names are the one class (Plan decision 16).

Reply scheduling (spec §9.2, complete):

```python
def schedule_reply(state: GameState, pid: str, thread_id: str) -> None:
    """On a player send: a read, then (decided at the read) a reply or left-on-read; one decision per burst."""
    person_state = state.social["people"][pid]
    if person_state.get("pending_read"):
        return                                    # a burst: the read already queued decides for all of it
    person = population.profile(state, pid)
    arch = behaviour.archetype(person.archetype)
    now = state.world_clock_hours
    rng = behaviour.person_rng(state, SOCIAL_PACE, pid)
    read_at = now + behaviour.lognormal_minutes(rng, *arch.read_minutes) / 60.0
    if behaviour.status_at(person, read_at) == "asleep":
        read_at = behaviour.next_awake(person, read_at)
    person_state["pending_read"] = True           # inside the social_message handler that called this
    events.push(state, read_at, pid, "read", thread_id=thread_id)


@events.handles("read")
def _on_read(state: GameState, event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    pid, thread_id = event["pid"], event["thread_id"]
    person_state = state.social["people"][pid]
    person_state["pending_read"] = False
    apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_read", "thread_id": thread_id, "reader": pid, "at": event["at"]})
    person = population.profile(state, pid)
    arch = behaviour.archetype(person.archetype)
    rng = behaviour.person_rng(state, SOCIAL_BEHAVIOUR, pid)
    interest = float(person_state.get("interest", 50))
    burst = _player_burst(state, thread_id)
    ghost_p = arch.ghost_per_exchange * (1 - interest / 100.0) * _stage_factor(person_state.get("stage"))
    if rng.random() < ghost_p:
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_person", "pid": pid, "set": {"ghosted": True}})
        events.cancel(state, pid)
        return {"ok": True, "ghosted": True}
    if rng.random() >= arch.reply_prob * _interest_factor(interest) * _burst_factor(burst):
        return {"ok": True, "left_on_read": True}
    pace = behaviour.person_rng(state, SOCIAL_PACE, pid)
    minutes = behaviour.lognormal_minutes(pace, *arch.reply_minutes) * _status_mult(person, event["at"]) / max(0.2, interest / 50.0)
    decision = Decision(situation="reply", kind="text", max_bubbles=min(3, 1 + burst))
    queue_due(state, pid, thread_id, decision, event["at"] + minutes / 60.0)
    return {"ok": True, "reply_in_minutes": minutes}
```

(`_interest_factor`, `_burst_factor`, `_status_mult`, `_stage_factor` read their numbers from `behaviours.yaml` `pacing:` -- add that block; rule 10.) All handlers above run INSIDE `social_event`'s handler, so their writes are under rule 3 (the `pending_read` flag set through the dict is a write inside a social effect handler -- acceptable: `social_event` is the effect; record this in `events.py`'s docstring: "an event handler writes `state.social` directly; it IS the effect").

Initiations and double texts (spec §9.3): the `daily` hook draws each live person's `initiations` (`SOCIAL_BEHAVIOUR`), queueing `initiate` events inside their free hours whose `when` holds; on player silence ≥ `double_text.after_hours` a `double_text` is queued, capped by `max_in_row`. Receipts (§9.4): on a `receipts: true` app the `read` handler writes `read_at` on the player's messages; `open_thread` writes the player's read time and, if receipts are on, the person "sees" it (`seen_by_them_at`); left-on-read costs interest at `left_on_read_sensitivity` per hour, applied at that person's next event (the cost lands in T9's `social_regard`; T8 records `left_on_read_since` and T9 reads it -- T9's test asserts the cost). Numbers (§9.5): `ask {what: number}` → `social_ask` → `messaging.ask_number(state, pid)`: legal at stage `talking`; the decision is a draw (`SOCIAL_BEHAVIOUR`) on `trust/100 × archetype.media.accept_mult`, taken when they next read; yes opens the numbers, no answers "not yet" as a due message (`situation: answer_request`). T9 puts the §10.3 gate in front of the draw (`gate_holds(state, pid, "numbers")`) and adds the `exclusive` kind; T9's test covers the gate both ways. On accept, T8 writes the stage through `social_person`'s `set` (`{"stage": "numbers", "stage_since": now}`); T9 moves every stage write to its own `social_stage` kind and proves with an AST scan that none is left (Plan decision 17). The Texts and Natter threads open (`texts:<pid>`, `natter:<pid>`).

**Interfaces:**

The regard clamp (spec §10.2, complete):

```python
@effect_kind("social_regard")
def _e_regard(state: GameState, effect: dict[str, Any], ctx: EffectContext) -> dict[str, Any]:
    pid = effect["pid"]
    person_state = state.social["people"][pid]
    person = population.profile(state, pid)
    arch = behaviour.archetype(person.archetype)
    rules = social.data("relationships")["regard"]
    day = int(effect.get("day", state.world_day))
    if person_state.get("regard_day") != day:
        person_state["regard_day"] = day
        person_state["regard_today"] = {"warmth": 0, "trust": 0}
    today = person_state["regard_today"]
    w_lo, w_hi = rules["per_reply"]["warmth"]
    t_lo, t_hi = rules["per_reply"]["trust"]
    warmth = _clamp(int(effect.get("warmth", 0)), w_lo, w_hi)
    trust = _clamp(int(effect.get("trust", 0)), t_lo, t_hi)
    if warmth > 0:
        warmth = round(warmth * arch.warmth_mult)
        warmth = min(warmth, max(0, int(rules["per_day_gain"]["warmth"]) - today["warmth"]))
    if trust > 0:
        trust = min(trust, max(0, int(rules["per_day_gain"]["trust"]) - today["trust"]))
    today["warmth"] += max(0, warmth)
    today["trust"] += max(0, trust)
    cap = _band_cap(arch.warmth_cap_band)            # dry texter never passes "warming" (data)
    start = rules["start"]                            # where regard starts: data, never a literal (F20)
    person_state["warmth"] = _clamp(int(person_state.get("warmth", start["warmth"])) + warmth, 0, cap)
    person_state["trust"] = _clamp(int(person_state.get("trust", start["trust"])) + trust, 0, 100)
    if "interest" in effect:                          # the engine's own interest moves (§10.2's last bullet)
        person_state["interest"] = _clamp(int(person_state.get("interest", 50)) + int(effect["interest"]), 0, 100)
    mood = effect.get("mood")
    if mood in social.data("relationships")["mood"]["values"]:
        person_state["mood"] = mood
        person_state["mood_until"] = state.world_clock_hours + float(social.data("relationships")["mood"]["decay_hours"])
    person_state["rev"] = int(person_state.get("rev", 0)) + 1
    _maybe_record_fight(state, pid)                   # warmth -8 or worse in one day -> a history row
    return {"type": "social_regard", "ok": True, "text": "regard", "applied": {"warmth": warmth, "trust": trust}}
```

(`rev` bump: every `social_*` handler that writes `people[pid]` bumps `rev` -- add a helper `touch(person_state)` in `relationships.py` and call it from T7/T8's handlers too, editing them in this task; T14's commit reads `rev` only for logging.)

Proposals (§10.5): a table in `relationships.yaml` `proposals:` mapping each id to its gate (stage and gate name); `legal_proposals` returns those whose gate holds; `apply_proposal`: `offer_number` → `social_request` (from them, `what: number`); `ask_meet` → request (venue picked in T24: until then `ask_meet` is not in the data table -- T24 adds the row and its handler, and `legal_proposals` only returns rows present in data, so T24 adds it); `offer_photo/voice/video` → T19 adds those rows likewise; `invite_game` → T22; `ask_exclusive` → request; `cool_off` → `social_regard {interest: -min(5, arch.cool_off_step)}`. **T9 ships exactly three rows: `offer_number`, `ask_exclusive`, `cool_off`**; T19 adds `offer_photo`, `offer_voice`, `offer_video`, T22 `invite_game` and T24 `ask_meet` (nine in all).

Ask pacing (§10.6): `asks_today[kind]` per person per day; the second ask that day multiplies accept odds by `asks.repeat_mult` and costs `asks.repeat_warmth`; the third is refused by the catalogue ("Give it a day").

Arcs: the person's arc point drawn (`SOCIAL_BEHAVIOUR`) in their first `social_person` write (edit T7's handler); `turner` queues an `arc_beat` at the exchange count reached; `arc_beat` writes the canon row id to `canon`, applies `then` through `social_stage` and `social_regard`, and sets the next due message's decision `reveal` (queue a `reply`-situation due message with `situation: reveal_turn`). `drifter_fade` is a `daily_hooks` entry.

```python
    _act(receipts_state, "ask", target=thread, args={"what": "number"})
    _act(receipts_state, "ask", target=thread, args={"what": "number"})
    third = _act(receipts_state, "ask", target=thread, args={"what": "number"})
    assert third["refused"] and third["result"]["error"] == "Give it a day"

def test_every_stage_write_goes_through_social_stage() -> None:
    """Plan decision 17. Anchored at the repo (a wrong cwd must not pass vacuously) and widened to any
    `[...]["stage"] = ...` outside `_e_social_stage` -- T7's `social_match` wrote one directly (F18)."""
    import ast
    from pathlib import Path

    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "engine" / "social"
    files = sorted(root.rglob("*.py"))
    assert len(files) >= 10, root                         # the package is there: the scan is not vacuous
    for path in files:
        tree = ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        allowed = {line for fn in ast.walk(tree)
                   if isinstance(fn, ast.FunctionDef) and fn.name == "_e_social_stage"
                   for line in range(fn.lineno, (fn.end_lineno or fn.lineno) + 1)}
        for node in ast.walk(tree):
            if isinstance(node, ast.Dict):
                keys = [k.value for k in node.keys if isinstance(k, ast.Constant)]
                if "type" in keys and "set" in keys:
                    type_value = node.values[keys.index("type")]
                    set_value = node.values[keys.index("set")]
                    if getattr(type_value, "value", "") == "social_person" and isinstance(set_value, ast.Dict):
                        set_keys = [k.value for k in set_value.keys if isinstance(k, ast.Constant)]
                        assert "stage" not in set_keys, f"{path}:{node.lineno} writes a stage through social_person"
            if isinstance(node, (ast.Assign, ast.AugAssign)) and node.lineno not in allowed:
                targets = node.targets if isinstance(node, ast.Assign) else [node.target]
                for target in targets:
                    if (isinstance(target, ast.Subscript) and isinstance(target.slice, ast.Constant)
                            and target.slice.value == "stage"):
                        raise AssertionError(f"{path}:{node.lineno} writes a stage outside social_stage")


def test_no_event_kind_is_queued_without_a_handler(receipts_state, matched, caplog) -> None:
    from engine.game import clock

    matched("matchstick")
    clock.advance_time(receipts_state, 24 * 30)
    assert "No handler for queued event" not in caplog.text
```

**Interfaces:**

- Produces:

  - `saves.save_now(session, *, entry: Optional[dict] = None) -> bool` (CALLER HOLDS the turn lock; clears `dirty`; False and no write for an `ending` session -- checked FIRST, since the spill writes before `_autosave` could refuse -- or a `keeps_nothing` store; otherwise `archive.spill(session)` then `_autosave(..., record=entry is not None, entry=entry)` behind the `owner_open` check of `session.store`)

`DirtySaver` (complete):

```python
class DirtySaver:
    """
    One thread and a heap of (due, seq, session, epoch) (spec §17.2). Marking a
    session dirty schedules it at now + social.autosave_seconds. When due:
    liveness, a NON-BLOCKING try of the turn lock (busy -> again in 1 s), save
    if still dirty, release, end_after_turn. So a swipe with no follow-up is on
    disk within autosave_seconds (plus any busy retry).
    """

    BUSY_RETRY_SECONDS = 1.0

    def __init__(self, *, clock: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic) -> None:
        self._lock = threading.Lock()            # LEAF: guards the heap only
        self._wake = threading.Event()
        self._heap: list[tuple[float, int, Any, int]] = []
        self._seq = itertools.count()
        self._clock = clock
        self._stopped = False
        self._thread: Optional[threading.Thread] = None

    def schedule(self, session: Any, due: float) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            heapq.heappush(self._heap, (due, next(self._seq), session, session.epoch))
            start = self._thread is None
            if start:
                self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, name="social-dirty-saver", daemon=True)
        if start:
            self._thread.start()
        self._wake.set()

    def _pop_due(self) -> Optional[tuple[Any, int]]:
        with self._lock:
            if self._heap and self._heap[0][0] <= self._clock():
                _due, _seq, session, epoch = heapq.heappop(self._heap)
                return session, epoch
            return None

    def _next_wait(self) -> float:
        with self._lock:
            return 5.0 if not self._heap else max(0.0, self._heap[0][0] - self._clock())

    def _run(self) -> None:
        while not self._stopped:
            item = self._pop_due()
            if item is None:
                self._wake.wait(self._next_wait())
                self._wake.clear()
                continue
            session, epoch = item
            if not alive(session, epoch) or not session.dirty:
                continue
            if not session.lock.acquire(blocking=False):
                self.schedule(session, self._clock() + self.BUSY_RETRY_SECONDS)
                continue
            try:
                if session.dirty:
                    save_now(session)
            finally:
                release_turn(session)                 # the one release (Plan decision 31)

    def stop(self) -> None:
        self._stopped = True
        self._wake.set()
```

`save_now` (complete -- the two checks of spec §16.5):

```python
def save_now(session: Any, *, entry: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None) -> bool:
    """Save one phone session. CALLER HOLDS session.lock."""
    from engine.scenes.default_state import _autosave

    if getattr(session, "ending", False):
        # _autosave refuses an ending session too, but the archive spill below runs BEFORE it and
        # would re-create a purged save folder (R22): refuse here, first.
        logger.info("[social] Not saved: the session is ending (operation=save_now, id=%s)", session.session_id)
        return False
    if keeps_nothing(session):
        session.dirty = False                      # a harness store: nothing to write (Plan decision 28)
        return False
    store = getattr(session, "store", None) or _default_store()
    if session.owner and store.owner_open is not None and not store.owner_open(session.owner):
        logger.info("[social] Not saved: the account is closed (operation=save_now, id=%s)", session.session_id)
        return False
    archive.spill(session)                         # Plan decision 18: before the envelope is replaced
    _autosave(session, "", "", record=entry is not None, entry=entry)
    session.dirty = False
    return True


def mark_dirty(session: Any, reason: str) -> None:
    session.dirty = True
    if keeps_nothing(session):
        return                                     # no timer and no DirtySaver thread for a harness (F3)
    get_dirty_saver().schedule(session, time.monotonic() + float(social.setting("autosave_seconds", 10)))
```

`release_turn` (in `engine/session/store.py`, Plan decision 31; `saves.py` re-exports it with `from engine.session.store import release_turn`):

```python
def release_turn(session: GameSession) -> None:
    """Let go of a turn lock: release it, then run end_after_turn if an admin end is waiting (v0.20.0 T15)."""
    session.lock.release()
    after = getattr(session, "end_after_turn", None)
    if after is not None:
        after()
```

`run_guarded`'s `finally` becomes `release_turn(session)` (behaviour byte-identical; `tests/test_admin_model.py` and the purge tests guard it).

`flush_taken(session)`: `if session.dirty: save_now(session)` -- called (a) in `SessionStore.sweep_idle` for each stale session after its pop, still holding its lock, before `_release`; (b) in `_build`'s `for other in released:` loop before `_release` (hosted one-run-per-account). Both behind `if social.declared()`. Never in `end`/`end_owner` (they set `ending`; `_autosave` refuses anyway). `flush_all()`: every live session of the store, try-lock, `flush_taken`. Wired to shutdown: local -- `atexit.register(saves.flush_all)` when the phone routes register (T14 extends it to cancel passes first); hosted -- `engine/hosting/__init__.py`'s shutdown path (read its `shutdown`/`drain` callers; call `flush_all` after `drain`).

`run_mechanics` step 4 (insert before the emit; `_e_settings` gains the fields `last_active_wall` and, in T16, `away_since`):

```python
    # 4. Mark dirty and stamp; save at once after a match, a stage change, an ending or an away advance.
    from engine.social import saves

    apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_settings", "field": "last_active_wall", "value": time.time()})
    reasons = {r.get("save_reason") for r in _effects_of(receipt)} & saves.IMMEDIATE
    if reasons:
        saves.save_now(session, entry={"phone": intent.get("action", ""), "day": state.world_day,
                                       "hour": state.world_hour})
    else:
        saves.mark_dirty(session, "action")
```

(`social_settings` gains `field: last_active_wall`; `last_active_wall` is a wall stamp, excluded from the replay comparison. Each handler that is a save trigger returns `"save_reason": "match" | "stage" | "ended"` in its result dict; `_effects_of(receipt)` walks the skill result's `effects` list -- make every phone skill return its applied effects' results under `effects`.)

The archive (**Plan decision 18**): `save_now`, under the turn lock and immediately before `_autosave`, calls `archive.spill(session)` (it reads `session.engine.state` and `session.saves.path_for(session.save_id)`; there is no `spill(state, save_dir)`), which takes each thread's messages past `social.state_messages` out of state through `apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_message", "op": "archive", "thread_id": ..., "count": n})` (the handler returns the removed rows and adds `n` to `thread["archived"]`) and appends them to `<save dir>/phone_archive.jsonl` (`append_jsonl`) BEFORE `_autosave` replaces the envelope. A crash between the two leaves the rows in both the old envelope and the file: a duplicate line, deduplicated by message id on read. `SaveStore.save` itself is unchanged (Plan decision 18). Paging: `archive.page` reads state's rows then the file's (deduped, newest first, `before` an id, `limit` ≤ 50); the route resolves the session (owner-checked by `store.require`) and its save directory (`session.saves.path_for(session.save_id)`). `SaveStore.delete` already removes every file in the save directory, `phone_archive.jsonl` included (asserted below); phone MEDIA live under the storage root's `media/phone/<owner>/<save_id>/` and T18 adds their deletion.

The purge test: copy the admin-end setup from the existing test that proves "a purge is never undone by the turn that was still running" (`Grep "purge" tests/test_admin*.py`), make the session dirty with a phone action, end the account, purge its folder, then fire the timer (`DirtySaver` with a zero delay), call `flush_all()` and `sweep_idle(ttl_minutes=1)` (`sweep_idle` floors its TTL at one minute, `store.py:905`, so 0 would mean 1 anyway), and assert the account's save folder DOES NOT EXIST (`not folder.exists()`: nothing re-created it). Helpers added to `tests/social_fixtures.py` in this task: `_live_session() -> (scene, session)` (a local app with the story active and one run), `_phone(scene, session, action, target="", args=None, text="")` (posts `/api/phone/action`), `_matched_in(session, app) -> thread_id`, `_wait_until(predicate, timeout=2.0)` (polls every 20 ms, fails on timeout -- a bounded wait, never a bare sleep). The deck's top card is T7's `_top(state, app)` (no twin helper); `_with` is T9's. Each test here calls `saves.reset_dirty_saver()` in its teardown (a fixture in this file) so no DirtySaver heap outlives its test.

### Task 11: Agreement (§11.5), every check whose state exists now

**Files:**
- Create: `engine/social/agreement.py`, `tests/test_social_agreement.py`, `tests/fixtures/social_agreement/` (one YAML of fixtures per check: `good:` and `bad:` lists)
- Modify: `games/read-receipts/data/social/relationships.yaml` (`claims:` word lists per stage, if T9 left them thin; `declines:` and `media_claims:` phrase lists, `media_denials:`), `engine/games/validation.py`
- Docs: story CHANGELOG; AUTHORING note deferred to T31

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: T3 (`Person`, `population.profile`), T8 (contacts = every other pid with a thread), T9 (`STAGES`, `stage_index`).
- Produces:
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) class Failure: check: str; where: str; index: int; detail: str` (`where` is `bubbles|shared|learned`; `index` the item's position)
  - `check(reply: dict[str, Any], decision: Decision, view: AgreementView) -> list[Failure]` -- PURE (**Plan decision 24**: the spec's `check(reply, decision, state, pid)` is `check(reply, decision, view_from_state(state, pid))`)
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True) class AgreementView: stage: str; person: Person; player: dict; contacts: tuple[str, ...]; agreed_meets: tuple[dict, ...]`
  - `view_from_state(state: GameState, pid: str) -> AgreementView` -- built HERE (pre-flight F11: everything it reads exists by T11; `agreed_meets` reads `state.social["meets"]`, empty until T24), used by T14's commit re-check; `view_from_snapshot` is T13's
  - `view_of(case: dict) -> AgreementView`, `decision_of(case: dict) -> Decision` (from a fixture dict; public: T13, T14 and T26 reuse them)
  - `STATE_CHECKS = frozenset({"stage_claimed", "another_person", "meeting_claimed"})` (re-run at commit, §16.3 step 3)
  - check names: `media_claimed`, `media_denied`, `stage_claimed`, `profile_contradicted`, `another_person`, `number`, `decline_wording` (T24 adds `meeting_claimed`)

The checks are PURE over `(reply, decision, view)` -- no state, no RNG -- so T14 runs them on its snapshot holding no lock (**Plan decision 24**). Add `test_view_from_state_matches_the_live_person(receipts_state, matched)`: after a match, `view_from_state(state, pid)` carries that person's stage, profile and the other thread holders' names as `contacts` (never the person themself). Each is a function registered in `CHECKS: dict[str, Callable[[dict, Decision, AgreementView], list[Failure]]]` by a `@check_fn(name)` decorator (T24 adds one more by decoration). Precision first (A2's rule: a check that fires on good prose is worse than none):

```python
@check_fn("media_claimed")
def _media_claimed(reply: dict[str, Any], decision: Decision, view: AgreementView) -> list[Failure]:
    """They say media was sent and none is attached (§11.5)."""
    if decision.media_kind:
        return []
    out = []
    phrases = _phrases("media_claims")            # relationships.yaml: "sent you a pic", "listen to this", ...
    for i, bubble in enumerate(reply.get("bubbles") or []):
        text = _norm(bubble.get("text", ""))
        hit = next((p for p in phrases if _whole_phrase(p, text)), None)
        if hit:
            out.append(Failure("media_claimed", "bubbles", i, hit))
    return out


@check_fn("another_person")
def _another_person(reply: dict[str, Any], decision: Decision, view: AgreementView) -> list[Failure]:
    """A name from the player's contacts this person cannot know (cast.py's surface forms reused)."""
    from engine.agents.cast import _surface_forms

    forms = {f.lower(): name for name in view.contacts for f in _surface_forms(name, name)}
    out = []
    for where in ("bubbles", "shared", "learned"):
        for i, item in enumerate(reply.get(where) or []):
            text = item.get("text", "") if isinstance(item, dict) else str(item)
            tokens = set(re.findall(r"[a-z']+", text.lower()))
            hit = next((form for form in forms if form in tokens and form != view.person.name.lower()), None)
            if hit:
                out.append(Failure("another_person", where, i, forms[hit]))
    return out
```

(`_surface_forms` is a private function of `engine/agents/cast.py`; import it by name -- the reachability scan counts a qualified import as a caller -- or lift it to a public `surface_forms` with the same body and keep the private name as an alias; choose the lift, with a one-line test in `tests/test_cast_criterion.py` that the alias and the public name agree.) The other checks: `stage_claimed` (any word list in `claims:` of a stage AFTER `view.stage` appears as a whole phrase); `profile_contradicted` (their age as a number other than `person.age` next to "I'm"/"im"/"I am"; a job label from `population.yaml` other than theirs after "I work as"/"I'm a"; a neighbourhood other than theirs after "I live in"; a `learned` fact containing the player's own age/job/neighbourhood stated otherwise); `number` (`len(bubbles) > decision.max_bubbles`); `media_denied` (media attached and a `media_denials:` phrase present); `decline_wording` (decision `kind == "decline"` and a bubble matches an `agree_phrases:` list -- "sure", "ok sending", "here you go"). Phrase lists are story data (rule 12: the words are the story's register; the engine only matches them).

```python
"""Each agreement check fails its bad fixtures and passes its good ones (spec §11.5, A2)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from engine import yamlio
from tests.social_fixtures import receipts_active  # noqa: F401

FIXTURES = Path(__file__).parent / "fixtures" / "social_agreement"
CHECKS = ["media_claimed", "media_denied", "stage_claimed", "profile_contradicted",
          "another_person", "number", "decline_wording"]


def _cases(name: str, which: str) -> list[dict]:

@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", CHECKS)
def test_every_bad_fixture_fails_its_check(receipts_active, name) -> None:
    from engine.social import agreement

    for case in _cases(name, "bad"):
        failures = agreement.check(case["reply"], agreement.decision_of(case), agreement.view_of(case))
        assert any(f.check == name for f in failures), case


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", CHECKS)

    from engine.social import agreement

    for case in _cases(name, "good"):
        assert agreement.check(case["reply"], agreement.decision_of(case), agreement.view_of(case)) == [], case

def test_checks_are_pure(receipts_active) -> None:
    import ast

    tree = ast.parse(inspect.getsource(agreement))
```

`summarize` rewritten over `summarize_with` (its messages byte-identical -- the LM Studio golden pins them):

```python
def summarize_with(existing, items, *, renderer, fallback, system, new_label, llm_fn, max_words):
    if not items:
        return existing, False
    if llm_fn is None:
        return fallback(existing, items), False
    messages = [
        {"role": "system", "content": system},
        {"role": "user", "content": f"CURRENT SUMMARY:\n{existing or '(nothing yet)'}\n\n{new_label}:\n{renderer(items)}"},
    ]
    try:
        result = (llm_fn(messages) or "").strip()
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 -- summarization is best-effort
        logger.warning("[memory] Summarizer failed (operation=summarize_with): %s", exc)
        result = ""
    if not result:
        return fallback(existing, items), False
    return _within_words_text(result, max_words), True
```

(`summarize` keeps its own logging lines and ledger writes and calls `summarize_with(ledger.summary, evicted, renderer=_render_turns, fallback=_fallback_summary, system=SUMMARIZER_PROMPT.format(max_words=max_words), new_label="NEW EVENTS", llm_fn=llm_fn, max_words=max_words)`. Read `summarize`'s tail -- after `used_llm` -- and keep every behaviour; `_within_words_text` is whatever it applies to `result` today.)

The summary prompt (§11.6): `memory.summary_messages` returns exactly `[{"role": "system", "content": <thread_summary.md verbatim>}, {"role": "user", "content": "CURRENT SUMMARY:\n...\n\nNEW MESSAGES:\n..."}]` and `summarize_thread` passes `system=<thread_summary.md>` and `new_label="NEW MESSAGES"` to `summarize_with`, so the two agree by construction.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: T3, T8 (`Decision` in `kinds.py`), T9 (`stage_words`, `band`, history, `legal_proposals`), T11 (`agreement.check`, `AgreementView`), T12 (`memory.budgeted`, canon, knows, summary).
- Produces: `PersonSnapshot`, `VoicedReply`, `LLMFn` (shared interfaces), and:

  - `voice(snap: PersonSnapshot, llm_fn: Optional[LLMFn]) -> VoicedReply`

  - `tests/social_stub.py::StubVoice(policy: str = "warm", *, block: Optional[threading.Event] = None, fail: int = 0)` -- a callable `LLMFn` returning schema-valid JSON by policy (`warm`: regard +3/+1 `fond`; `cold`: -2/-1 `annoyed`; `zero`: 0/0 `neutral`, no proposals; `wordy`: different words, same regard as `zero`), counting its calls; `block` makes each call wait on the event (bounded `wait(5)`, failing if it times out); `fail` makes the first N calls return text that does not parse.

The schema (§11.2, complete):

```python
def reply_schema(decision: Decision, proposals: tuple[str, ...], moods: tuple[str, ...]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    props: dict[str, Any] = {
        "bubbles": {"type": "array", "minItems": 1, "maxItems": max(1, decision.max_bubbles),
                    "items": {"type": "object", "additionalProperties": False,
                              "properties": {"text": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 280}},
                              "required": ["text"]}},
        "regard": {"type": "object", "additionalProperties": False,
                   "properties": {"warmth": {"type": "integer", "minimum": -4, "maximum": 4},
                                  "trust": {"type": "integer", "minimum": -2, "maximum": 2},
                                  "mood": {"type": "string", "enum": list(moods)}},
                   "required": ["warmth", "trust", "mood"]},
        "learned": {"type": "array", "maxItems": 2, "items": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 140}},
        "shared": {"type": "array", "maxItems": 2, "items": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 140}},
        "proposals": {"type": "array", "maxItems": 2, "items": {"type": "string", "enum": list(proposals)}}
                     if proposals else {"type": "array", "maxItems": 0},
    }
    required = ["bubbles", "regard", "learned", "shared", "proposals"]
    if decision.media_kind:
        props["media"] = {"type": "object", "additionalProperties": False,
                          "properties": {"caption": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 200}},
                          "required": ["caption"]}
        required.append("media")
    return {"type": "object", "additionalProperties": False, "properties": props, "required": required}
```

(The ±4/±2 bounds here are the SCHEMA's; the engine's per-reply clamp reads `relationships.yaml` at commit -- T9 -- so a story narrowing them is honoured, and the schema is never the only guard. Confirm `engine.llm.schemas.conform` handles `maxItems: 0`; if not, omit `proposals` from `props` when empty and treat it as `[]`.)

The prompt (§11.3, complete):

```python
BLOCK_TITLES = ("WHO YOU ARE", "WHERE YOU ARE WITH THEM", "WHAT YOU KNOW ABOUT THEM",
                "THE CONVERSATION", "RIGHT NOW", "FORMAT")


def messages(snap: PersonSnapshot) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    """Exactly seven blocks, in order (spec §11.3, pinned by test_the_voice_prompt_is_exactly_its_blocks)."""
    texter = texter_prompt()
    blocks = memory.budgeted({
        "WHO YOU ARE": _who(snap),
        "WHERE YOU ARE WITH THEM": _where(snap),
        "WHAT YOU KNOW ABOUT THEM": _knows(snap),
        "THE CONVERSATION": _conversation(snap),
    }, window=int(social.setting("voice.window_tokens", 4096)))
    fixed_now = _right_now(snap, texter)
    fmt = render_format_block(reply_schema(_decision(snap), snap.proposals, _moods()))
    user = "\n\n".join([
        f"WHO YOU ARE\n{blocks['WHO YOU ARE']}",
        f"WHERE YOU ARE WITH THEM\n{blocks['WHERE YOU ARE WITH THEM']}",
        f"WHAT YOU KNOW ABOUT THEM\n{blocks['WHAT YOU KNOW ABOUT THEM']}",
        f"THE CONVERSATION\n{blocks['THE CONVERSATION']}",
        f"RIGHT NOW\n{fixed_now}",
        f"FORMAT\n{fmt}",
    ])
    return [{"role": "system", "content": texter.persona}, {"role": "user", "content": user}]
```

(Block 1 is the system message, `texter.md`'s persona section verbatim; blocks 2-7 are the user message's six titled sections. Add `voice.window_tokens: 4096` to `config/default.yaml`'s `social.voice`. `_right_now` ends with `SITUATION: <texter.instructions[decision.situation]>`; an unknown situation id is a load-time `ValueError` from `texter_prompt()`'s parse when any data names one the table lacks -- `check_social` cross-checks `behaviours.yaml` `instruction:` ids and every engine-emitted situation (a module constant `SITUATIONS` in `voicing.py`) against the table.)

`voice` (complete -- prompt, call, conform, agreement, one retry, fallback; pure over the snapshot):

```python
def voice(snap: PersonSnapshot, llm_fn: Optional[LLMFn]) -> VoicedReply:
    decision = _decision(snap)
    schema = reply_schema(decision, snap.proposals, _moods())
    if llm_fn is None:
        return fallback_reply(snap, "no model")
    msgs = messages(snap)
    view = agreement.view_from_snapshot(snap)
    failures: list[str] = []
    for attempt in range(2):
        try:
            raw = llm_fn(msgs, response_format(schema))
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 -- no server, a starved reply: the fallback
            logger.info("[social] Voice call failed (operation=voice, pid=%s): %s", snap.pid, exc)
            return fallback_reply(snap, "call failed")
        parsed = _parse(raw)
        if parsed is None:
            failures.append("unparsed")
            continue
        reply = conform(parsed, schema)
        found = agreement.check(reply, decision, view)
        if not found:
            return _voiced(snap, reply, fallback=False, failures=failures)
        failures.extend(f.check for f in found)
        if attempt == 0:
            msgs = msgs + [{"role": "assistant", "content": raw},
                           {"role": "user", "content": _correction(found)}]
            continue
        trimmed = _drop(reply, found)          # drop the offending bubbles/facts
        if trimmed.get("bubbles"):
            return _voiced(snap, trimmed, fallback=False, failures=failures)
    return fallback_reply(snap, "two failures", failures=failures)   # the record of why survives (F9)
```

`_correction(found)` is ONE line naming what to fix in engine words ("Your reply said a photo was sent; none was. Rewrite it without saying so.") -- built from `found[0].check`, never mentioning content rating (rule 12). `fallback_reply(snap, why, *, failures=())` picks a line from `texter.md`'s `fallbacks[archetype][situation]` (falling back to `[archetype]["reply"]`, then a one-word engine line) with `stable_rng(snap.seed, f"{SOCIAL_BEHAVIOUR}:fallback:{message_id}")` (`message_id` the first due message's id) -- no state counter, so a pure function of the snapshot; regard zero, nothing learned, `fallback=True`, `failures=list(failures)`. `production_llm_fn()`:

```python
def production_llm_fn() -> LLMFn:
    from engine.llm.backend import get_backend

    def call(msgs: list[dict[str, Any]], fmt: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        reply = get_backend().chat(msgs, profile="texter", response_format=fmt, reasoning="off", label="social_voice")
        if reply.truncated and not reply.content.strip():
            raise RuntimeError("starved")
        return reply.content
    return call
```

(`LMSResponse.truncated` is `finish_reason == "length"`, `engine/llm/events.py:116`; the starved case is "content empty at length".)

`SessionInbox` (complete):

```python
class SessionInbox:
    """
    Results that could not take a session's turn lock (spec §16.4): a media
    hand-off, a commit past its retries. One per GameSession OBJECT; its lock
    is a LEAF (its holder takes nothing). Drained under the turn lock by the
    next mechanics action and the next pass's snapshot, each item re-validated.
    A released session's inbox dies with it (NOT WIRED row: its voiced replies
    are re-voiced from the save's due messages; its media rows resubmit).
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._items: deque[dict[str, Any]] = deque()

    def put(self, item: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self._items.append(item)

    def drain(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        with self._lock:
            items = list(self._items)
            self._items.clear()
        return items

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        with self._lock:
            return len(self._items)
```

`GameSession` additions (none saved): `voicing: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)`, `typing: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)`, `inbox: Any = field(default_factory=_new_inbox)` where `_new_inbox` imports `SessionInbox` lazily (store.py must not import `engine.social` at module top: a non-social story's session still gets an inbox object -- cheap, and never used; record that in the field's comment).

The pass (complete -- the generation check, admission, snapshot, call, commit):

```python
_PASS_IDS = itertools.count(1)


@dataclass
class VoicePass:
    session: Any
    epoch: int
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: f"v{next(_PASS_IDS)}")
    cancel: CancelToken = field(default_factory=CancelToken)
    attempts: int = 0
    deadline: float = 0.0
    rerun: bool = False


def _retry_ms() -> list[int]:
    return [int(x) for x in social.setting("voice.commit_retry_ms", [50, 100, 200, 400, 800])]


def _try_lock(session: Any, cancel: CancelToken) -> bool:
    """The turn lock, NON-BLOCKING, on the commit_retry_ms schedule (never a blocking take)."""
    for delay in [0] + _retry_ms():
        if cancel.cancelled:
            return False
        if delay:
            cancel_wait(cancel, delay / 1000.0)        # Event-based wait that returns early on cancel
        if session.lock.acquire(blocking=False):
            return True
    return False


_release = saves.release_turn        # the one release (Plan decision 31): unlock, then end_after_turn


def run_pass(p: VoicePass, *, llm_fn: Optional[LLMFn], admit: bool, store: Any = None) -> str:
    session = p.session
    # THE GENERATION CHECK, first of four (before admission, snapshot, each commit).
    if p.cancel.cancelled or not saves.alive(session, p.epoch, store):
        return "cancelled"
    started = time.monotonic()
    p.deadline = started + _deadline_seconds()
    admission = None
    if admit:
        from engine.llm.gate import InferenceBusy, admit_turn

        try:
            admission = admit_turn(
                timeout=float(social.setting("voice.admit_wait_seconds", 5)),
                label="voice",
                account=str(session.owner or ""),
                deadline_seconds=_hosting_deadline(),
                cancel=p.cancel,
            )
        except InferenceBusy:
            return "busy"                              # nothing marked yet: the caller re-queues or drops
    try:
        if not saves.alive(session, p.epoch, store) or p.cancel.cancelled:
            return "cancelled"
        if not _try_lock(session, p.cancel):
            return "busy"
        try:
            if not saves.alive(session, p.epoch, store):
                return "cancelled"
            _drain_inbox(session)                      # each item re-validated (§16.4)
            snaps = snapshot(session, p)               # READ, then MARK in session.voicing
        finally:
            _release(session)
        outcome = "ok"
        for i, snap in enumerate(snaps):
            if p.cancel.cancelled or not saves.alive(session, p.epoch, store):
                return "cancelled"
            if time.monotonic() >= p.deadline:
                reply = voicing.fallback_reply(snap, "deadline")
            else:
                session.typing.add(snap.thread_id)     # read by every payload built meanwhile (§9.4)
                try:
                    reply = voicing.voice(snap, llm_fn)
                finally:
                    session.typing.discard(snap.thread_id)
            if p.cancel.cancelled:
                return "cancelled"                     # an answer that came back after cancel is discarded
            result = commit(session, p, reply, store=store, last=(i == len(snaps) - 1))
            if result == "ended":
                return "cancelled"
            if reply.fallback:
                outcome = "fallback"
        if admission is not None:
            admission.release()
            admission = None
        _summarise_one(session, p, store=store)        # at most one utility call (one-argument fn, F10), holding no lock
        return outcome
    finally:
        if admission is not None:
            admission.release()
        _clear_marks(session, p)                       # under the lock if free now, else left to the next holder
```

`_clear_marks(session, p)` iterates `tuple(session.voicing.items())` and drops this pass's entries; every payload builder reads `tuple(session.typing)` -- a pass thread mutates both holding no turn lock, so no reader iterates the live container (pre-flight F22; no per-session lock is added). `_summarise_one(session, p, store)` picks at most one thread whose overflow is due, snapshots it under the lock, calls `memory.summarize_thread(view, _summary_fn or memory.production_summary_fn())` holding no lock -- the summary function takes ONE argument, the messages, never the voicer's two-argument `LLMFn` (pre-flight F10) -- and commits the text through `social_memory {op: summary}` under the lock again, after the generation check.

`snapshot` (CALLER HOLDS the lock): picks due messages by §11.1's priority (`thread.focus` desc, then the person's warmth desc, then age asc), at most `social.voice.max_per_pass` PEOPLE, one thread each, skipping messages already in `session.voicing` (another live pass's) and fallback-voiced ones; for each builds a `PersonSnapshot` (deep copies: `copy.deepcopy(people[pid])`, the thread tail, the summary, `relationships.legal_proposals(state, pid)`, the player's profile, `now` with the person's status, the contacts' display names); then `session.voicing[mid] = p.id` for each message taken. If more due messages remain, `p.rerun = True`.

`commit` (complete -- §16.3):

```python
def commit(session: Any, p: VoicePass, reply: VoicedReply, *, store: Any = None, last: bool = False) -> str:
    if not _try_lock(session, p.cancel):
        if not saves.alive(session, p.epoch, store):
            return "ended"
        session.inbox.put({"kind": "voiced", "payload": reply, "epoch": p.epoch,
                           "account": session.owner, "pass": p.id})
        return "inbox"
    try:
        # 1. Liveness.
        if not saves.alive(session, p.epoch, store):
            return "ended"
        return _apply_reply(session, p.id, reply, last=last)
    finally:
        _release(session)


def _apply_reply(session: Any, pass_id: str, reply: VoicedReply, *, last: bool = False) -> str:
    """§16.3 steps 2-5, under the turn lock. Shared by commit and the inbox drain."""
    state = session.engine.state
    # 2. Still wanted?
    thread = state.social["threads"].get(reply.thread_id)
    person = state.social["people"].get(reply.pid) or {}
    wanted = [m for m in (thread or {}).get("messages", [])
              if m["id"] in reply.message_ids and m["voiced"] is False
              and session.voicing.get(m["id"]) == pass_id]
    if thread is None or not wanted or person.get("unmatched_by") or person.get("blocked") or person.get("ended_by"):
        logger.info("[social] Reply discarded (operation=commit, pid=%s, rev=%s)", reply.pid, person.get("rev"))
        _report_pass(session, "discarded")
        return "discarded"
    # 3. Re-check what depends on state.
    proposals = [x for x in reply.proposals if x in relationships.legal_proposals(state, reply.pid)]
    bubbles = list(reply.bubbles)
    if not reply.fallback:
        view = agreement.view_from_state(state, reply.pid)
        decision = Decision(**wanted[0]["decision"])
        found = [f for f in agreement.check({"bubbles": [{"text": b} for b in bubbles],
                                             "shared": reply.shared, "learned": reply.learned},
                                            decision, view) if f.check in agreement.STATE_CHECKS]
        drop = {f.index for f in found if f.where == "bubbles"}
        bubbles = [b for i, b in enumerate(bubbles) if i not in drop]
        if not bubbles:
            reply = voicing.fallback_reply_for(state, reply.pid, reply.thread_id, wanted, "state changed")
            bubbles, proposals = list(reply.bubbles), []
    # 4. Apply, in one transaction.
    with transaction(state) as tx:
        apply_effect(state, {"type": "social_voiced", "thread_id": reply.thread_id,
                             "message_ids": [m["id"] for m in wanted], "bubbles": bubbles,
                             "caption": reply.caption, "fallback": reply.fallback})
        if not reply.fallback:
            relationships.apply_regard(state, reply.pid, reply.regard, day=state.world_day)
            memory.add_canon(state, reply.pid, reply.shared)
            memory.add_knows(state, reply.pid, reply.learned)
            for name in proposals:
                relationships.apply_proposal(state, reply.pid, name)
        tx.commit()
    for m in wanted:
        session.voicing.pop(m["id"], None)
    # 5. Save (the pass's last commit, under this lock: §17.2) or mark dirty; push.
    if last:
        saves.save_now(session)
    else:
        saves.mark_dirty(session, "voiced")
    _push_message(session, reply.thread_id, [m["id"] for m in wanted])
    _report_pass(session, "fallback" if reply.fallback else "ok")
    return "committed"
```

(`fallback_reply_for(state, pid, thread_id, wanted, why)` is a T13 helper building a snapshot-free fallback from live state -- add it to `voicing.py` in this task with a test. `social_voiced` (this task's handler) fills `bubbles` and sets `voiced: True` or `"fallback"` on the wanted messages, and for a decision with `media_kind` attaches the caption to the media row -- T19 reads it. The pass's last commit saves under its own lock (`last=True`, §17.2: "at the end of each voicing pass that committed anything"); an inbox drain's `_apply_reply` uses the default `last=False` and leaves the save to the action's own step 4.)

The scheduler: a bounded deque of `VoicePass` (`social.voice.queue_max`, oldest dropped with a log line), one entry per live session OBJECT (a `request` for a session already queued or in flight sets `rerun` instead), `social.voice.workers` daemon threads started lazily, the leaf `_lock` over the deque and the in-flight table `{(session_id, epoch): VoicePass}`, a `threading.Condition` on that lock for waking. A worker pops, runs `run_pass(p, llm_fn=_llm_fn or production_llm_fn(), admit=hosting_enabled())`, and on `busy` re-queues with backoff `2 ** attempts` seconds (2, 4, 8, 16, 32) as a timed entry, dropping it after `voice.max_attempts` with a `dropped` metric; on `ok`/`fallback` with `rerun`, re-queues at once; on `cancelled`, drops every reference. `forget(session_id)`: cancel every queued or in-flight pass whose `(session_id, epoch)` no longer matches a live store entry. `cancel_all()`: cancel everything (drain, shutdown). `in_flight()`: passes currently in `run_pass`.

`run_until_quiet(session, llm_fn)`: `while messaging.unvoiced(state) and passes < max_passes: run_pass(VoicePass(session, session.epoch), llm_fn=llm_fn, admit=False)`, synchronously, no scheduler (spec §16.2) -- the replay test, the simulator and `screenshot_runs.py` use it.

Wiring: `routes.py`'s phone handlers call `voicer.request(session)` after `run_guarded` returns when `messaging.unvoiced(session.engine.state)`; `routes.register` calls `voicer.set_emitter(scene.emit_to_session)`; `SessionStore._release` calls `voicer.forget(session_id)` behind `if social.declared()`; `engine/hosting/__init__.py::drain`:

